- **Each splash gets its OWN clipPath id.** An SVG `clipPath` is addressed document-wide, so two splashes on one page both resolved to the first one's clip and the second never filled in. `splashMarkup()` mints a fresh id per call rather than trusting there is only ever one on screen.
- **It is body-mounted, so every colour carries a hard fallback** (`var(--ncm-primary, #F65261)` and friends) — there is no `.ncm-app` ancestor to inherit from if the player theme has not written `:root` yet. Same rule as the chat cards.
- **`pointer-events: none`.** A splash is a flourish, not a modal; an overlay that eats clicks can hold the table hostage if anything goes wrong.
- **`FADE_MS` and the CSS transition on `.ncm-boot-splash.fade-out` are two halves of one timing** — the JS removes the node after the fade, so they must agree. The sim reads the number out of the stylesheet and compares it.
- **Motion is vetoed, never raised:** the module's animation preference (`themeService.getAnimationLevel()`) and the OS `prefers-reduced-motion` can each force the still frame; neither can turn motion back on. The wrapper in `registerMessagingSystem` answers only those two Foundry questions (may we move, what version are we) and hands off.
- Why it is a module rather than a function inside `registerMessagingSystem`: so **`dev/mockups/boot-splash-preview.html` imports the REAL thing** (play, scrub to any `?t=`, three frozen frames, the reduced path) instead of a hand-copied lookalike that drifts from what ships. Sim: `dev/tools/sims/test-boot-splash.mjs` (19 checks).

## Architecture: message threading / conversation view

Clicking the **`Thread · N`** pill in the message detail (a `<button data-action="toggle-thread">` in the subject strip) swaps the single-message detail pane for a **conversation transcript** — every message in the thread, oldest→newest, on a timeline rail, with the message you came from highlighted and auto-centered. Back returns to the single message. This is the only "Option C" full-transcript view; it lives entirely inside `message-detail.hbs` as an `{{else if message.threadExpanded}}` branch of the locked/encrypted/normal conditional chain (no separate template/part).

**Thread state is a single instance flag.** `MessageViewerApp._threadExpanded` (default false) is flipped by `_toggleThread()` and **reset to false in `_selectMessage`** so opening any message — including an entry's "open" button inside the transcript — returns to the single-message view. `_getEnrichedMessage` attaches `threadInfo` (badge count), `threadExpanded`, and `threadEntries` (only built when expanded). `_onRender` centers `.ncm-viewer__thread-entry--current` in `.ncm-viewer__thread-scroll`. Per-entry bodies are independently access-checked via `messageAccessService.checkAccess` so encrypted/network-locked messages stay redacted *inside* the transcript, exactly as in the single view.

**Thread membership is resolved, not a raw `threadId` filter.** `scripts/utils/threading.js` (pure, no Foundry globals) merges a flat message list into conversations with a union-find over **four signals**, any one of which links two messages:
1. `threadId` — canonical group id
2. `inReplyTo` — points at the parent's id
3. `references[]` — full ancestor chain (survives forward-of-forward, reconstructs even if `threadId` is lost)
4. **subject + shared counterparty** — fuzzy fallback: identical `normalizeSubject()` (strips `RE:`/`FWD:`/`FW:`) **and** an overlapping `counterparties()` (the *other* party from the viewing actor's perspective — the actor's own ids are excluded, since they're in every message). Rescues forwards-to-strangers, hand-composed same-subject mail, and legacy pre-`references` messages.

Signal 4 is the only one that can over-merge (two unrelated "FWD: invoice" chains from the same fixer). It's gated by the world setting **`subjectThreading`** ("Smart Conversation Grouping", default on; strings in `en.json`). Off = strict reply/forward-link threading only. Both `_getThreadInfo` and `_buildThreadEntries` route through the resolver via the shared `_threadOpts()` helper. IDs are compared on their **base** form — `baseId()` strips a trailing `-sent` so a sent copy and its received twin count as one identity. Within a single actor's mailbox each logical message appears once (the twin lives in the other actor's inbox), so no dedup is needed.

**Forwards now connect by design.** `MessageService.buildForward` carries `threadId` + `inReplyTo` + `references` (the new recipient simply sees a single-message thread until they reply); `buildReply` carries `references` too. Both build the chain via `_buildReferences(originalMessage)` (appends the base parent id to the de-duped ancestor list). `sendMessage` never leaves `threadId` undefined — it falls back `references[0] → inReplyTo → messageId`. `references` is persisted/read by `MessageRepository` (`createMessage` flags + `_pageToMessage`), defaulting to `[]` for old messages. **Note:** `MessageRepository.getThread()` (used by the macro API) still keys purely on `threadId` — it was left for back-compat and is *not* the resolver; the transcript UI does not use it.

## Architecture: responsive message viewer

The Message Viewer adapts to the viewer's **own width** via CSS **container queries** — `.ncm-viewer { container-type: inline-size }` (message-viewer.css §23), not viewport `@media`. Breakpoints: wide >860 (full two-pane), medium ≤860 (icon-only header buttons, avatar-only identity, HUD drops Location), narrow ≤520 (single column; pill→dot+%; primary tabs + category pills icon-only via the `font-size:0` collapse trick; denser rows). The single-column switch is the only JS: `_onRender` sets `data-pane` on `.ncm-viewer` (`selectedMessageId ? 'detail':'list'`); narrow CSS shows one pane and the `.ncm-viewer__mobile-back` button (action `mobile-back`). Window min-size clamped in a `setPosition()` override. When narrow, the list-panel inline `width` is overridden with `width:100% !important` (placed after the `--list-collapsed` rule to win on specificity/order).

The header-condense breakpoint is **860, not 720** (raised): the full header layout (identity name+email, net pill, clock, scene, 4 action buttons) needs ~860px, so between 720–850 the flexible parts (identity name, scene) collapsed to zero and the **fixed** parts (avatar, divider, net-dot, action icons) overlapped — the expand-chevron painting over the net pill's dot, buttons spilling off the right. Condensing at 860 skips that dead band. (The single-column switch stays at 520.) Verified by rendering the header at a range of widths via headless Chrome — the reliable way to debug these container-query bands without launching Foundry.

**Network selector shows only reachable networks.** `MessageViewerApp._buildSelectorNetworks()` still computes each network's `state` ('active'|'available'|'locked'|'unavailable') but then `.filter()`s out `unavailable` and 0% signal — like real life, you don't see networks you can't reach. `locked` (in range, needs auth) stays visible. `availableCount` derives from the shown list. Dead zones return `[]` (jammed state); a scene with networks configured but none reachable shows a "No Networks in Range" empty state (distinct from the dead-zone state) in `network-selector.hbs`.

**Network selector dropdown rows (the "Available Networks" popup).** Redesigned for legibility (`network-selector.css` / `network-selector.hbs`): each row carries `--net-c` (its configured color) inline; the **left accent stripe + active-row tint + 4-bar signal meter** all use that color (active row glows, locked rows mute the bars to grey). The right column = a **status chip** (`Connected` / `Connect` / `🔒 Locked`) over the signal bars. Bar thresholds match the rest of the app (80+%=4 bars, 50–79=3, 25–49=2, 1–24=1). Sizes were bumped from the old 7–11px (name 11→14, meta 7→9.5, icon 26→32, header/footer 8→9).

## Architecture: network authentication dialog

`NetworkAuthDialog` (`scripts/ui/NetworkManagement/NetworkAuthDialog.js` + `templates/dialogs/network-auth-dialog.hbs` + `styles/components/network-auth-dialog.css`) is the player-facing gate shown when connecting to a secured network **and now for terminal account logins** (via `showForAccount` — see the terminal Auth section; both go through the `SecurityAuthAdapters` source seam, so the dialog itself is source-agnostic). It's a **method-card** layout: Password / Skill Check / Key Item each render as a stacked card, **all visible at once** (so AND-mode can show every requirement). GM bypass (`gmBypass`) skips auth entirely — that's why solo-GM testing never sees the gate.

- **Two auth logics** (`network.security.authLogic`): `any` (OR — any one method grants) vs `all` (AND — every enabled method required). Derived in `_prepareContext`: `showPolicy` (banner only when >1 method), `progressLabel` ("Step X of N"), `activeMethod` (first incomplete card, AND only), per-method `passwordNum/skillNum/keyItemNum`, `showOrBeforeSkill/KeyItem` (OR dividers). AND-mode cards are **numbered ①②③** with `REQUIRED → PASSED/ACCEPTED` chips; completing one collapses it and advances the step counter (`_checkAuthComplete`). OR-mode cards are split by OR dividers, no numbers.
- **States the template must keep:** the Black-ICE danger panel (`isLethalICE` → pulsing red, shows ICE name/class/`formula`; `iceDealsDamage` from `failureMode==='damage'` drives the per-skill `⚠ fail = NdN to HP` warning), error banner (shake), key-item drop zone (browse + presented-item + wrong-item error), and the full **lockout** takeover (countdown + spent pips). Top accent bar color is set by `_onRender` via `data-auth-state` (password=gold, skill=purple, keyitem=cyan, **ice=red**, lockout=red).
- **Skill resolution goes through `SkillService` — never the dialog's old manual lookup.** The old code called `skillService.getSkillBreakdown`/`resolveSkillBonuses`, **which don't exist**, so it silently fell back to a buggy manual path (hardcoded INT for display; dropped the stat in the roll when the skill item wasn't matched → "total 3 with INT 8"). Now `_breakdownForSkill` uses **`SkillService.getSkillData`** (display) and the roll uses **`SkillService.performCheck`** (the canonical CPR path shared with shard hacking — posts the skill-check/"Network Bypass" chat card, handles crit/fumble/Luck). Both resolve identically, so display and roll can't disagree. Each skill **row shows its own stat+level** (`TECH 7 + Skill 4 → 11`); the breakdown grid renders only for single-skill networks (`showBreakdownGrid`), since it can only represent one skill.
- **Two skill-name footguns, both fixed in `SkillService._readSkillData`:** (1) **Role abilities** like **Interface come from the Netrunner role item, not a `type:'skill'` item** — `_readRoleAbility` resolves the rank from the role (main ability / sub-abilities / role name / `interface→netrunner` fallback). **The CPR data model stores `system.mainRoleAbility` as a STRING ("Interface") with the rank in `system.rank`** — the lookup only handled an `{name, rank}` object, so Interface resolved only when the role item was literally named "Netrunner" (fixed 2026-10-29, dev: "not detecting my player's Interface rank"; checks in test-jack-in §7). This matters everywhere Interface is the default breach skill (shard hack, contact breach, message decrypt). (2) **Name matching is space/punctuation-insensitive** (`_normSkill`) so a config value like `"Electronics/SecurityTech"` resolves to the actor's `"Electronics/Security Tech"` item. Stat linkage falls back to `SKILL_MAP` when neither item nor role supplies one. **On save**, network configs canonicalize typed skill names via `canonicalizeSkillName()` (constants.js — normalized → proper `SKILL_MAP` key) so newly-stored data matches CPR item names; the read-time normalization covers legacy data. Interface is rolled as **TECH + Interface-rank + d10** per the existing `SKILL_MAP` (intentional module choice, not RAW Interface-only).
- **CSS gotcha (bit us):** the Foundry button reset must be scoped to the inner **`.ncm-auth-dialog`**, NOT the bare `.ncm-network-auth-dialog` root — a blanket `button` reset also hits the window-frame **close (✕) control** and breaks its FontAwesome glyph. Button visual props need `!important` (Foundry + the CPR system both style buttons aggressively). The dialog is non-resizable, so `.window-content` has `max-height: 82vh; overflow-y: auto` + a styled scrollbar so a tall AND-mode dialog can't run off-screen.

## Architecture: the minimize button (2026-10-29)

Dev: "the admin panel needs a minimize option along with all the popups … cyberpunk-like but mostly clear". Most NCM windows were already `minimizable` — by double-clicking the title bar, which nobody knew. `scripts/utils/windowMinimize.js` (`registerMinimizeControls`, registerUIComponents init/21) adds a visible button beside the ✕ from TWO hooks, no per-window code: `renderApplicationV2` (Foundry fires `render<Class>` for every class in the chain) for every `ncm-app` frame with `window.minimizable`, and `renderDialog` for the bigger v1 dialogs (`ncm-themed-dialog`, `ncm-pkr-dialog`, `ncm-rmgr-dialog`, `ncm-time-config-dialog`, `ncm-ap-dialog`) — **never a yes/no confirm** (`themedConfirm` adds `ncm-dlg-confirm`). Pure `wantsMinimize` / `minimizeFace` (a centred `fa-minus`, NOT `fa-window-minimize` whose bar sits low and reads off-centre; `fa-window-restore` when rolled up). A MutationObserver on the frame's `minimized` class keeps the glyph right after a title-bar double-click; the button swallows its own dblclick and carries NO `data-action` (Foundry would look for a handler). Now minimizable: Configure Shard, the entry composer, Configure Terminal, Create Network. **Deliberately not:** the network login dialog, the dead-zone warning, email setup (mid-flow). CSS at the end of `styles/base/window-chrome.css` (`.ncm-minbtn`: window tokens, corner brackets + glow on hover, a rolled-up window gets a 3px accent strip on its title bar). Sim `dev/tools/sims/test-window-minimize.mjs` (21). Not live-tested.

## Architecture: UI apps

All NCM windows extend `BaseApplication` (`scripts/ui/BaseApplication.js`), which is `HandlebarsApplicationMixin(ApplicationV2)`. BaseApplication provides:

- `subscribe(event, callback)` — managed EventBus subscriptions auto-cleaned in `close()`
- `_debouncedRender()` — 150ms coalesced render for bursty event sources
- `_applyAtmosphere()` — sets `data-ncm-scanlines/neon/animation-level` attributes on the root
- `playSound(id)` / `playEffect(el, cls, ms)` — respect user prefs
- `_animationActive` flag — set by handlers during multi-step flag writes to suppress debounced re-renders mid-animation

**Always use `_onRender` for post-render DOM work.** Never override `render()`. ApplicationV2 template parts must have a single root element.

## Architecture: AdminPanel composition

`scripts/ui/AdminPanel/AdminPanelApp.js` is a thin shell (~1,000 lines). Tab logic lives in **7 separate files** under `scripts/ui/AdminPanel/tabs/`:

```
AdminPanel/
├── AdminPanelApp.js     ← lifecycle, services, action dispatchers, EventBus, keyboard
├── BaseTab.js           ← abstract: prepareContext / onRender / onClose + service proxies
└── tabs/
    ├── OverviewTab.js   ContactsTab.js   MessagesTab.js
    ├── NetworksTab.js   ShardsTab.js     SpamTab.js   ToolsTab.js
```

Tabs are **composed, not inherited** — `AdminPanelApp` holds instances in `this.tabs = { overview, messages, ... }` (lazy-init via getter). Tab classes own their own state (`this._search`, etc.), data gathering, DOM wire-up, and action-handler implementations.

**Why static action handlers stay on `AdminPanelApp`**: Foundry's ApplicationV2 binds the `static DEFAULT_OPTIONS.actions` map at class load. Static methods must remain on the parent. Each handler body is a **one-line delegator**:

```js
static _onContactFilter(event, target) {
  this.tabs.contacts.onFilter(event, target);
}
```

Inside the static method, `this` is the `AdminPanelApp` instance (Foundry binds it). Inside the tab method, `this` is the tab instance.

**Adding a new admin panel action:**
1. Add the action name → static handler in `DEFAULT_OPTIONS.actions`
2. Add a one-line static delegator method on `AdminPanelApp`
3. Implement `onXyz(event, target)` on the appropriate tab

**Cross-references from a tab to parent state/methods** go through `this.app.X`:
- `this.app._saveScroll()` — preserves admin-content scroll
- `this.app._activeTab` — currently active tab key
- `this.app._animationActive` — suppress debounced renders during multi-step writes
- `this.app._relativeTime(iso)`, `this.app._getRelativeTime(ts)` — formatters
- `this.app.constructor._findItem(id)` — static item lookup (used by ShardsTab, NetworksTab)

## Architecture: Admin → Overview — THE NOW LINE (2026-10-28)

Rebuilt from the dev-approved **Version B** of `dev/mockups/admin-overview-rethink-mock.html` (frame A, the "control room", is a rejected record — keep it). The six-section stack of 2026-10-26 (quick-action row, NEEDS YOU list, AT THE TABLE scene bands, tiles row, the runway, Recent activity, players' characters, NPCs) is GONE; **one vertical timeline owns the tab, with a dock on the right.** The rules stay pure in `scripts/utils/overviewModel.js`; `OverviewTab` only gathers and wires; `prepareContext` returns `{ ov: null }` unless the Overview is the active tab, and the template keeps the shared `.ncm-tabhd` page head. Context = `ov: { clock, time, date, sceneName, sceneDead, nl, table, tiles }`.
- **The instrument (`.ncm-ov2-nl*`)** — a time gutter + a track; `nowLineLayout({activity, needs, timers, nowMs, zoom, open, sel, off, trackPx, clock})` lays it out. A fixed **NOW line at 38%** (`NOW_PCT`). **ABOVE it, the past**: the merged activity rows (`mergeActivity` of saved mail + the Security `traceLog` + the session `logActivity` rows, the `_feedFilter` still narrows them) placed by how long ago on the GAME clock on a **log scale** (`pastPct`: the first hour gets the most room, 24 h is the top edge), newest nearest the line, rows past an hour fade (`is-far`), each in its tone (`ACTIVITY_TONES`: a failed roll red · a success green · mail amber · shard hue-2 · a session event act). **ON the line, the needs-you queue folded into chips by KIND** (`needChips` over `rankNeeds` — the ranking, `dismissKey` and per-signature dismissal are unchanged; `NEED_CHIPS` gives each kind its label / icon / tone: every security kind red, waiting / setup / bricked amber); a chip click (`ovNeedFilter` with the kind, `_needFilter`; the same chip or `all` folds it) hangs a panel under the line (`openItems`, `openTop`) with that kind's ranked items — the same need rows, action buttons (`dataAttrs`), per-item dismiss and Dismiss all — and the future rows dim and go inert behind it. **BELOW it, the future**: every timer from `_gatherTimers` (shape unchanged: `{id, kind, at, name, subline, lines, actions}`) placed by time-to-go (`futurePct`, linear to the zoom's edge), zoom **1h / 6h / 24h** (`RUNWAY_ZOOMS`, `rwZoom`), the first 10 min tinted red and the first hour amber (`zoneBad` / `zoneAttn`, `timerUrgency` colours the dot), each row `<kind> <name>` + its countdown + **its own existing action buttons**; a row click (`rwPick`, toggles `_rwSel`) opens its facts (`lines`) in place; past the edge or with no room = **"+N later"** (zooms out via `widerZoom`), likewise **"+N earlier"** at the top (→ Security tab). A legend strip of kind keys with counts hides a kind (`rwLane`, `_rwOff`) and carries the summary (N under 10 min · N this hour · N timers). **Rows never overlap: `spreadRows` pushes them apart by one row-gap (`NL_ROW_PX / trackPx`), the first row on either side sits 1.5 gaps off the line (room for two rows of chips), the outer edge keeps a gap for the "+N" chip, and what does not fit folds into the count** — `OverviewTab.TRACK_PX` (600, pop-out 680) must match the CSS grid height (656 / 736 minus the key lines; the sim checks the pairing). `RUNWAY_OPS` stay `rwZoom / rwLane / rwPick / rwLift / rwClose` (Lift = `SecurityService.clearFor`, Close = `TerminalService.removeAccount`); every other button is an existing handler (Messages `schedSendNow` / `stopTimer` via tabAct, `editScheduled`, `cancelScheduled`, `securityCountdownCancel`, `hardDeleteMessage`, `securityEscalate`…). **The DOM clock** (`_tickRunway`, every 15 s while `.ncm-ov2-nl[data-nl-zoom]` is on screen, cleared on render/close) moves every future row up by the game time elapsed since the render (`data-top` − drift, so the push-apart spacing holds), hides landed ones, ticks every countdown / ago label and the NOW clock — never a re-render.
- **The dock (`.ncm-ov2-dock`, scrolls in its own box)**: the **clock card** (`formatCyberDate` time + date, the viewed scene in its hue, red + a `dead zone` pill when it is one) · **At the table, compact** (`ov.table.players`, a flat list online-first: face in the name hue + online dot + amber unread badge, name, `player · scene`, held / no address in words, the network with signal bars in the network's hue — red `no signal` on a dead zone, `—` not reported, `offline`; the head says "N need you" or "N online"; click → `tblToggle` opens the why panel under the row: `assessPlayer` facts + fixes through the unchanged `TABLE_OPS`; `_gatherTable` still returns the scene `bands` for the sims and any other reader) · **Quick** (Write = `openComposer`, Contact = `openGMContacts`, Broadcast = `ovBroadcast` → the Networks tab, Set time = `openTimeSettings`; **2026-10-28, dev-agreed: Inbox = `qInbox` (a picker over the players' characters → `openInbox`), Push contact = `qPush` (directory picker → character picker → `pushToPlayer`), New shard = `qShard` (ShardsTab `_createBlankShard`), +1 h / +1 day = `qClock` (manual → `advanceManualTime`; world time / Simple Calendar → `game.time.advance`; real-time / disguised warn) — `QUICK_OPS`) · the six **tiles** (count, label, red "bad" line + red number) → `switchTab`.
- **The GM learns where players are from them**: the network socket's `'switch'` case records `game.nightcity.playerNetworks` (userId → `{networkId, at}`), a player sends a **`'hello'`** at postReady/40, and a GM reload sends **`'helloRequest'`** so players answer. **A hello records the network and does nothing else — it never emits `NETWORK_CHANGED`** (auto-spam would fire on every login). Signal shown = the network's configured strength.
- CSS `styles/components/admin-panel/tab-overview.css`, `ncm-ov2-*` + `--ncm-*` tokens only (no literal besides `#fff`), 2px radius, the `.ncm-ov2-mod` instrument frame with corner ticks; the old `ncm-ov2-rw*` runway CSS is deleted. Field names avoid helper names (`subline`, never `sub`). Measured with the preview's `?contrast=1` walk: **0 text nodes under 4.5:1 on clean-dark, clean-light and high-contrast** (faded `is-far` past rows are history and excluded). Sims `dev/tools/sims/test-overview.mjs` (351: the rules incl. chips / geometry / `spreadRows` / `nowLineLayout`, the real `_gatherTable` + every op, a real Handlebars render, CSS + action wiring, the hello) and `test-overview-runway.mjs` (118: the future half, the real `_gatherTimers`, the ops, the DOM clock, render + CSS). Preview `dev/mockups/admin-overview-nowline-preview.html?t=&zoom=&open=&sel=&off=&tbl=&pop=1&empty=1&dead=1&contrast=1` renders the REAL template over the REAL rules (`?ctx=<json>` takes a dump from `NCM_DUMP=<dir> node dev/tools/sims/test-overview.mjs`); `admin-overview-runway-preview.html` is gone with the runway. **Not built from the mock:** nothing — the mock's five chips, inline timer buttons, "+N later", the dock's four blocks and the player rows are all here; the activity rows show the row's subline as a tooltip rather than a second line (one line per row keeps the push-apart honest). **Not live-tested.**

## Architecture: GM Admin Panel theme (separate from player theme)

The Admin Panel has its **own** theme, isolated from the player theme. The player `ThemeService` writes `--ncm-*` to `:root`; `styles/themes/admin-theme.css` re-defines the same `--ncm-*` variables **on any element carrying `data-ncm-admin-theme`** (`:is(.ncm-app, .ncm-admin-dialog)[data-ncm-admin-theme]`; the bare `.ncm-app.ncm-admin-panel` is kept as a robust default), which wins for every descendant (custom properties resolve from the nearest ancestor) — so each tagged window is themed in isolation and `:root` is untouched. **Don't move these overrides to `:root`** (that's the opposite of the player-theme rule) and **don't scope them tighter than the window root** or descendants won't inherit them.

**Carry-over to every GM tool (2026-09-10, tester: "Light theme, open Full Editor → still dark").** `scripts/utils/adminTheme.js` is the ONE stamping path. Rule agreed with the dev: **GM tools follow the admin theme + text size; player-facing immersive windows (message viewer, composer, shard viewer, terminal, Contact Manager, Theme Customizer, Program Deck) stay on the player theme even on a GM client.**
- **AppV2 windows opt in with `static ADMIN_THEMED = true`** (BaseApplication default false). `BaseApplication._onRender` calls `_applyAdminTheme()` right after `_applyAtmosphere` (stamps the attribute, forces scanlines/neon off, applies the text-size zoom + one-time frame resize via `_applyAdminTextScale`). Flagged: AdminPanelApp (and its pop-out AdminTabWindow), GMContactManagerApp [retired 2026-10-27], ActorImportPickerApp, ContactShareDialog, NetworkManagementApp [retired 2026-10-27], CreateNetworkDialog, TerminalConfigApp, ItemInboxConfig, DataShardComposer (its `_onRender` skips super, so it calls `this._applyAdminTheme()` explicitly — keep that), MalwareWorkshopApp. A new GM window = add the flag; a subclass that overrides `_onRender` without super must call it by hand.
- **Foundry v1 `Dialog`s can't inherit a window's vars (body-mounted), so GM code opens them through `adminDialog` / `adminConfirm` / `adminPrompt` / `adminWait`** (same signatures as `new Dialog` / `Dialog.confirm` / `Dialog.prompt` / `Dialog.wait`), which add the `ncm-admin-dialog` class; the `renderDialog` hook (`registerAdminDialogHook`, registerGMTools ready/111) stamps the attribute + zoom and grows the width once. admin-theme.css carries explicit chrome rules for `.ncm-admin-dialog[data-ncm-admin-theme]` (frame/header/content/inputs/buttons, `!important` against core + CPR). All ~80 GM-tool call sites were swept (AdminPanel tabs, GM Contact Manager, Role Manager, Terminal/Shard config, entry composer, Network Manager, Workshop, ShardSheetOverride, DateRangePicker; `showWorldItemPicker` passes `admin:true` while the player inventory picker stays plain). **A `new Dialog(` in GM-tool code is a bug** — grep for it. **Player-facing windows have their own wrappers (2026-10-21, tester: "pop-up windows still not resizing for text")** — `playerDialog` / `playerConfirm` / `playerPrompt` / `playerWait` (same file) tag `ncm-player-dialog`; the same `renderDialog` hook zooms those by the PLAYER's Theme Customizer Text Size (read live from `:root`'s `--ncm-text-scale-base`, saved `playerTheme.textScale` as fallback, clamped to the slider's 80–150) and grows the width once, with **no palette change** (a body-mounted Dialog keeps Foundry's look by design). **A dialog follows its OPENER**: when `document.activeElement` sits inside a `[data-ncm-admin-theme]` window (GM-view viewer/composer/contacts, a config sheet) the player wrapper tags it admin instead — the CyberTimePicker rule, now generic (the picker's own `activeElement` check is gone). The 30 raw call sites in the viewer / composer / Contact Manager / shard viewer / terminal / Theme Customizer / CyberTimePicker / `showNamePicker({admin:false})` were swept; **a raw `new Dialog(` / `Dialog.confirm(` anywhere in `scripts/` is now a dialog that scales with nothing** — `dev/tools/sims/test-admin-theme.mjs` (61 checks) walks the tree and fails on one (exempt: the wrappers and the namespace-migration prompt, which runs before the hook exists). Not live-tested.
- **Brand hex → `--ncm-*` sweep**: `node dev/tools/sweep-brand-hex.mjs [--check] [--only <selector regex>] <css…>` rewrites the literal brand palette (variables.css defaults: `#19f3f7`→secondary, `#2a2a45`→border, `#1a1a2e`→bg-surface, `#555570`→text-muted, brand `rgba()`→`color-mix`) to variables; it skips `var(--x, #hex)` fallbacks and non-brand colours. Swept: gm-contact-manager, network-management, shard-config, create-network-dialog, role-manager, activity-log, actor-import-picker, cyber-time-picker, tab-tools/tab-security dialogs, and item-inbox.css **composer blocks only** (`--only`, so the player shard viewer + `.shard-preset-*` tokens are untouched). `--nm-net-*` network-TYPE colours were restored by hand — they're identity, not theme (standing rule). `--check` exits 1 if a swept file regains a brand literal. Malware Workshop needed nothing (its hexes are all `var(…, #fallback)`).
- **The sweep table has THREE tiers, and the first pass alone left "the configs looking rough" (dev, same day).** Tier 1 = the variables.css brand palette. Tier 2 = the near-brand neutrals the config sheets typed by hand (`#0e0e16`/`#0a0a12` section backgrounds, `#5a5a7a`…`#d0d0d8` greys) — left alone they stay near-black on the light preset while the text flips dark. Tier 3 = the terminal config's account editor shades in terminal.css (`#0b0b14`, `#22223a`, `#dde`, `#eef`…). Border declarations map neutrals to `--ncm-border-active` (`BORDER_OVERRIDES`). The tool also rewrites hardcoded `'Rajdhani'`/`'Share Tech Mono'`/`'Orbitron'` stacks to the `--ncm-font-*` slots. **Deliberately NOT mapped:** the boot/login/screensaver PREVIEW stages (`#050508`, `#07070c`…) — they mimic a terminal screen and must stay dark under any preset; status colours (trust-segment orange/green, locked purple, ICE red); `--*-net-*` declarations (`KEEP_DECL`). Guard placeholders are `@@G0@@`-style — the first version used ` 0 ` and would have swallowed `flex: 1 1 auto` (audited: no file was hit).
- Probe: `dev/mockups/admin-theme-carryover-preview.html?t=clean-light|clean-dark|high-contrast&zoom=130` renders a tagged AppV2 window + a tagged Dialog + an UNTAGGED control dialog against the real cascade and dumps computed vars into `<title>`/`#report`. **`dev/mockups/config-theme-probe.html?tpl=shard|terminal&t=…&tab=accounts`** renders the REAL `item-inbox-config.hbs` / `terminal-config.hbs` (partials auto-registered by their Foundry path names) through Handlebars with a permissive Proxy context (every branch shows, values print their path — a palette check, not a data check; the Proxy must use an object target + own-property descriptors + `toHTML`→undefined or Handlebars reads every value as empty) and reports low-contrast text nodes in `<title>`. Ignore hits whose background prints as `color(srgb … / 0.0x)` — translucent tints, not surfaces. Sim: `dev/tools/sims/test-admin-theme.mjs` (45 checks: wrappers, stamp/zoom, hook, flag inventory, immersive windows unflagged, CSS scope).
- Text size = CSS `zoom` on `.window-content` + frame growth (GM stylesheets are ~fixed-px with no `--ncm-text-scale`); `adminTheme.textScale` 100–150 via the Tools-tab chips.
- **GM view toggle (2026-09-10, dev: "toggle GM view / player view in the inboxes… the composer too, for readability").** Immersive windows are NOT `ADMIN_THEMED`, but `MessageViewerApp`, `MessageComposerApp` and `ContactManagerApp` carry **`static GM_VIEW_TOGGLE = true`**: a GM gets a header ⋮-menu entry ("Switch to GM view / player view") that flips the **client** setting `gmViewAdminTheme` (default **on**) and re-renders every open toggle window; on = admin preset + text size stamped on that window (`applyGmView`), off = the player theme (what the players see). Never affects players. Plumbing lives in BaseApplication (`_getHeaderControls` + `_onClickAction` + the `_onRender` branch) — **the composer does NOT extend BaseApplication**, so it mirrors those three hooks by hand; keep them in step. A subclass that overrides `_onClickAction` must call super or the toggle dies.
- **Third round (same day, GM-view live test) — four more, all now standing rules:** (1) **`_getHeaderControls` must return a COPY** — `super` hands back the shared `options.window.controls` array, and pushing into it appended one more "Switch to…" entry per render (the doubled menu item). (2) **AppV2 builds the frame ONCE, so a header-menu label never re-renders** — the toggle is therefore the injected **`.ncm-gmview-btn` header button** beside the close control (lit in the admin accent while GM view is on), patched by `syncGmViewControls` (adminTheme.js) on every render/toggle. **There is deliberately NO ⋮-menu entry** (dev: the eye button does the job; the menu duplicated it). JS-built dialogs with inline styles must use vars too — `DateRangePicker` (Tools → date range) was swept like MessagesTab's View Inbox. **Viewer pane stacking:** both panes are `isolation: isolate` stacking contexts, so the Dates/Sort/Filters dropdowns (z-index 30 inside the LIST pane) slid under the later-in-DOM DETAIL pane; message-viewer.css orders them (`list-panel z-index:2`, `detail-panel z-index:1`). (3) **The viewer/contacts/composer sheets carried a fourth palette** — Tailwind-ish `#00d4e6`/`#ef4444`/`#a78bfa`/`#34d399`/`#fbbf24` + neutral rgba triplets (`224,224,232`…) + `color:#fff` for the subject/header — tier 4 in the sweep tool (incl. a `WHITE_TEXT` rule: `color:#fff` → `--ncm-text-primary`, `color:` only). **NEAR-white was the hole in that rule** (2026-09-21): it matched pure white only, so `#f0f0f5` on `.ncm-viewer__subject-text` and `#f0f0f8` on the composer's bold text survived — and a near-white that never flips is exactly as unreadable on `clean-light` as a pure one. The message SUBJECT measured **1.05:1** against the light background (the dev: "white on white") and now reads 14.94:1. `WHITE_TEXT` now takes any `color:` literal whose channels are all ≥ 0xE0, and **`--check` reports them** — it used to scan only the brand HEX/RGB maps, so even a pure `color:#fff` could be reintroduced silently. Probe: **`dev/mockups/gmview-contrast-probe.html?t=clean-light|clean-dark|high-contrast|player`** renders the real markup against the real cascade and puts the worst text/background contrast ratio in `<title>` (it walks UP for the first opaque background — judging text against the translucent tint it sits on reports a false pass).. Players see no change (the vars resolve to the same brand colours under the player theme); under GM view the accents follow the preset. Swept: message-viewer / message-detail / message-viewer-hud / viewer-network-states / network-selector / contact-manager / message-composer / composer-atmosphere / theme-customizer / tab-messages / contact-verification / cyber-time-picker / **shard-sheet-badge** (it owns the `.ncm-pick-dialog` chrome that the email-domain and other pick dialogs wear — a class-collision source, all literal brand). (4) **Body-mounted popups inherit nothing** — `CyberTimePicker` tags its Dialog `ncm-admin-dialog` when `document.activeElement` sits inside a `[data-ncm-admin-theme]` window (a GM-view composer, a config sheet), so the picker matches its opener. **Theme Customizer** carries `GM_VIEW_TOGGLE` too; its `_applyLive` must skip `[data-ncm-admin-theme]` windows (it re-stamped scanlines onto the Admin Panel) and `_scopePreviewToPlayerTheme` re-applies the working prefs element-scoped onto `.ncm-tc__preview`/`.ncm-tc__fprev` so the preview shows the PLAYER palette while the chrome is admin. **Shard and terminal viewers deliberately have NO toggle** (dev: "I don't think the toggle needs to be on the shards"). Probe: `config-theme-probe.html?tpl=message-viewer/message-viewer.hbs&cls=ncm-message-viewer-window&f=isDeadZone,isEncrypted,…` — `?f=` forces named `{{#if}}` keys false so overlay states don't bury the list.
- **Second "still looks off" round (same day) — three token/scope bugs the sweep can't see:** (1) the Malware Workshop's rails/headers used **`--ncm-bg-mid`, which was never defined anywhere**, so 15 rules fell to their dark-slate fallback under every preset — now in variables.css (`#161622`) and all three admin presets. Run the undefined-var check (node one-liner in the session: every `var(--ncm-…)` name vs variables.css + admin-theme.css) when a themed window "ignores" the preset. (2) The **`--sp-*` shard tokens are defined on the shard viewer root only**; the entry composer and the workshop reuse `.ncm-shard-btn`/`.ncm-cl-*`/`.ncm-config-*` and got the dark fallbacks — item-inbox.css now mirrors the token block onto `.ncm-app.ncm-data-shard-composer, .ncm-app.ncm-malware-workshop`. (3) **JS-built dialogs with INLINE `style="color:#e0e0e8"`** (Messages → View Inbox) ignore every stylesheet — converted to `var(--ncm-*)`; grep `style="[^"]*#[0-9a-f]{6}` in `scripts/ui/AdminPanel` before adding one. Also: **scanline films drawn by `::after` regardless of the atmosphere attribute** (`.ncm-netmgr::after`, `.ncm-create-network-dialog::after`) are gated on `[data-ncm-scanlines="false"]` in admin-theme.css ("get rid of scanlines in subnet control"). **Heat rows name their object via `SecurityTab._heatObjectName`** (world item → actor-owned item "(owner)" → network → uuid → "(deleted object · id…)") — it used to print a raw id for anything but a world item. **Probe for JS-built windows:** `dev/mockups/workshop-theme-probe.html?t=…&lib=1` stubs enough Foundry to import the REAL `MalwareWorkshopApp`, construct it, and `_mount()` into the real cascade (the hbs is a 3-line shell, so the template probe shows nothing).

- **Config sheets sit one tier lighter** (2026-10-28, dev: Configure Terminal "reads more as high contrast than clean dark"): terminal.css / shard-config.css paint with `--ncm-bg-deep` / `-base`, which the admin aliases map to the near-black CANVAS, so admin-theme.css re-points them on `.ncm-app.ncm-item-inbox-config[data-ncm-admin-theme]` (deep→pane, base→pane-alt, mid→card) — both Configure Terminal and Configure Shard, every preset (high-contrast stays near-black on its own pane values). A new admin window built on `--ncm-bg-deep` for its BODY has the same problem; use the pane/card tokens.
- **Presets are pure CSS** keyed by `data-ncm-admin-theme="clean-dark|clean-light|high-contrast"` (no per-variable JS injection). `clean-dark` is the base rule (applies with no attr too, so it's the robust default); light/high-contrast are attribute overrides declaring only their palette diffs (glow/font overrides fall through from the base rule). Goal: clarity/accessibility/cleanliness — a calm slate dashboard, one restrained accent, **no neon/scanlines**, legible system UI fonts (mono kept for numbers).
- `AdminPanelApp._applyAdminTheme()` (called in `_onRender` **after** `super._onRender` so it beats `_applyAtmosphere`) sets the `data-ncm-admin-theme` attr from the `adminTheme` setting **and** force-sets `data-ncm-scanlines/neon="false"` regardless of player prefs.
- **Settings:** client-scoped `adminTheme` (`{ preset }`, `DEFAULTS.ADMIN_THEME`), with `SettingsManager.getAdminTheme/setAdminTheme` — fully independent of `playerTheme`.
- **Picker:** Tools tab "Admin Appearance" (`setAdminTheme` action → `ToolsTab.onSetAdminTheme` saves + re-renders; `ToolsTab.prepareContext` provides the preset list + active state).
- **Text size (2026-09-10, accessibility):** `adminTheme.textScale` (100/115/130/150, `ToolsTab.ADMIN_TEXT_SCALES`, chips under the preset picker → `setAdminTextScale`). The panel's CSS is ~500 fixed-px font sizes with no `--ncm-text-scale` plumbing, so `AdminPanelApp._applyAdminTextScale` sets a CSS **`zoom`** on `.window-content` and grows the window frame by the same ratio (tracked in `_appliedTextScale`) so the 820px layout keeps its proportions. Player-facing windows scale through the Theme Customizer's Text Size slider instead (range raised 120 → **150**).
- **Window-chrome controls follow the admin theme too.** Foundry colors the `.window-header .header-control` **icons** (`<i>`, close/etc.) near-white by default, and `window-chrome.css`'s `.header-control { color }` rule doesn't reach the inner `<i>`. That's invisible on the dark presets but **white-on-white on `clean-light`**. `admin-theme.css` forces both the button and its `> i` to `--ncm-text-secondary` (hover `--ncm-text-primary`; close-hover `--ncm-danger`), **scoped to `.ncm-app.ncm-admin-panel`** so the player theme's chrome is untouched.

**The admin panel has ONE visual vocabulary — copy it, don't invent one.** Both new tabs (Security, Terminals) shipped feeling foreign and the dev flagged it as "the fonts look off… doesn't match the other tabs". It measured out as two objective inversions of the shipped convention, so check yours before adding a tab:
- **Fonts: display-dominant chrome, mono for VALUES ONLY.** `grep -o "var(--ncm-font-[a-z]*" styles/components/admin-panel/tab-X.css | sort | uniq -c` — the shipped tabs run display:mono ≈ **59:5** (contacts), 31:3 (shards), 49:21 (messages). The new tabs were **inverted** (security 4:19, terminals 2:14) — mono on buttons/labels/badges/titles, which reads as a different app. This is just the standing font rule (Rajdhani chrome, Share Tech Mono for *technical data values*) applied: a person's name = display, a machine/file/ID/timestamp/roll/count = mono.
- **Radius is 2px. Everywhere.** Shipped tabs: 28×2px (contacts), 23×2px (shards), 31×2px (messages), 32×2px (networks). The new tabs used 5–9px + a 20px pill — rounded reads as a foreign UI next to sharp neighbours. Badges are **1px**.
- **Copy these three, they are the reference implementations:** button = `.ncm-spam-send-btn` (display 11/700, uppercase, `letter-spacing:.04em`, radius 2px, `appearance:none`, `min-height:0`); badge/chip = `.ncm-ct-badge` (display 9/700 uppercase .04em, radius 1px); **checkbox = `.ncm-ct-row__check`** (outlined box, radius 2px, checked = faint `secondary` fill + a **cyan ✓**). Note the module-global `.ncm-app input[type=checkbox]` (base/interactions.css) paints a *solid cyan box with a dark border-tick* — right for the player UI, foreign in the panel; override it per-surface. **`--checkbox-size` does NOT work for this** — Foundry core declares that var ON the input, so a value inherited from a wrapping label loses; size the box directly and neutralise BOTH the module's border-tick geometry and core's layered FA-glyph `content:"\f14a\f14a"`.
- Section titles: display 11–12px/700 uppercase `.06em` (`.ncm-war-section__title`); hints: display 9px/500 italic muted (`.ncm-war-section__hint`).

**Admin CSS must use `--ncm-*` vars, not hardcoded brand values, or it won't follow the admin theme.** A 2026-06 sweep converted ~600 solid brand hexes → `var(--ncm-*)` and ~380 brand `rgba(r,g,b,a)` → `color-mix(in srgb, var(--ncm-X) N%, transparent)` across all `admin-panel/tab-*.css` (color-mix is fine in Foundry v12's Chromium). When adding admin-panel styles, follow that discipline. **Intentionally left hardcoded:** network-**type** colors (CorpNet blues etc.), per-preset icon colors, and black/white `rgba` (shadows/overlays — theme-agnostic). Note: much shard-tab CSS reads `var(--sp-X, var(--ncm-Y))` / `var(--ncm-color-X, var(--ncm-X))` — `--sp-*` is undefined outside `.shard-preset-*` scopes and `--ncm-color-*` doesn't exist, so both **fall through to the themed `--ncm-*`**.

## Architecture: the 2026-10-27 admin redesign — shared plumbing

Every admin tab was re-mocked (`dev/mockups/admin-*-redesign-mock.html`, dev-approved) and rebuilt in one pass; the build brief is `dev/design/ADMIN-REDESIGN-BUILD.md`. **Not live-tested.** The shared pattern: sub-tabs with count badges · list + detail · edit in place · Pop out. Plumbing every tab uses:
- **`tabAct`** — `data-action="tabAct" data-owner="<tab key>" data-op="<op>"` → `this.tabs[owner].onAct(op, event, target)` (`AdminPanelApp._onTabAct`). New buttons use this; do NOT add a static delegator per button. Older static actions stay where other code/sims still call them.
- **Pop out** — `popOutTab` (`data-tab`, optional `data-select`) opens **`AdminTabWindow`** (`scripts/ui/AdminPanel/AdminTabWindow.js`): the SAME AdminPanelApp class pinned to one tab (id `ncm-admin-pop-<tab>`, 1120×780, root `.ncm-admin-panel--pop`, context `isPopout`, chrome hidden, `dockTab` = back to the panel). Tabs widen under `.ncm-admin-panel--pop` in their own CSS; number-key tab switching is off in a pop-out.
- **`game.nightcity.openAdminTab(tab, { popout, select })`** (registerGMTools) — the ONE launcher; `select` goes to the tab's **`openTo(select)`** before render. `openGMContacts`, `openNetworkManager*` and the scene-control button all route here.
- **Scope picker** — `scripts/utils/scopePicker.js` + `styles/components/scope-picker.css` (`ncm-scope-*`; mock `dev/mockups/scope-picker.js`): a searchable chip + popover for any list of world things that can grow (networks, scenes, people, identities) — search, group chips with counts, "has activity", pinned rows, multi (checkbox/Only/Clear/Done) or single. State lives in the module keyed by id; tabs use **`this.scopeId(name)`** (BaseTab — namespaced per window so the panel and a pop-out never share a selection), `ScopePicker.on(id, () => this.app.render())`, embed `{{{…}}}`, and call `ScopePicker.after()` in onRender (positions the popover). **Rule: a `<select>` is for a SHORT enum**; whole-world item/actor pickers stay on `showNamePicker`.
- Each tab's context lives under ONE namespaced key (`ct2`, `net2`, `msg2`, `term2`, `shards2`, …) because AdminPanelApp spreads every tab's context into one object; heavy builds run only while that tab is active.
- Retired windows: **`GMContactManagerApp`** and **`NetworkManagementApp`** (+ their templates/CSS) are DELETED — folded into their tabs.
- **A ScopePicker callback fires on EVERY change — opening the popover included** (`changed(id)` runs on the button click). A callback that closes the picker or re-renders unconditionally makes the list vanish the instant it opens (the Who knows whom Group picker did, 2026-10-28). Compare against the current state and return early when nothing changed. **And the popover REPAINTS ITSELF on every change** (`changed()` → `rerenderPop`): the host callback re-renders its window, which replaces the BUTTON but never the body-mounted popover, so a multi-pick's rows kept stale checks until reopened (dev 2026-10-28: "selecting works but it doesn't appear checked"). Probe `dev/mockups/scope-picker-probe.html` (served; drives the real module, title = OK/FAIL).
- **The scope picker's popover is a PORTAL** — `ScopePicker.html()` renders only the button; `after()` mounts the open popover on `document.body` (class `ncm-app` + the opener window's `data-ncm-admin-theme`, z-index 10000) and re-places it 40ms after every change even if the tab forgot `after()`. Rendering it inside the window let stacking/overflow/zoom bury it (tester: Send as "flashes behind the app").
- **Contrast pass (2026-10-29)** — 0 text nodes under 4.5:1 in clean-dark, clean-light and high-contrast across 41 real tab renders (Contacts, Messages, Overview, Access, Spam, the node inspector, the Sound board), measured with `dev/mockups/admin-contrast-check.html` (canvas-read colours, so oklab()/color-mix() are measured, translucent layers composited — the older per-preview walkers could not read oklab). Fixes: `--ncm-text-hint` / `--ncm-text-time` raised to ≥4.6 on every background up to hover (dark #99a2b2/#98a3b5, light #566476/#566477, still under the label tier); coloured TEXT on its own tint now follows the tag-pill rule (`color-mix(in oklab, <meaning> var(--ncm-ink), heading)`) — flags, alias names, waiting/problem counts, tallies, selected segments, links, initials (block at the end of admin-hierarchy.css); **`--ncm-ink-own`** (50 / 32 / 55%) for a tag whose colour is the user's own hex (group, network) and for msg2 flags on their tint; a red count badge reads in `--ncm-text-inverse`. New coloured text: use the ink mix, never the raw meaning colour.
- **The tab bar is GROUPED (2026-10-29, dev-picked "clustered tab bar", `dev/mockups/admin-nav-groups-mock.html`)**: one row, one click per tab, the tabs in labelled clusters — Overview · **COMMS** (Messages, Contacts, Spam; later Phone) · **NET** (Networks, Security; later Browser) · **WORLD** (Terminals, Shards; Access points next) · Tools (`.ncm-admin-tabs--grouped` > `.ncm-admin-tabgrp[data-tab-group]` with a caption; the open group's caption is lit). Every tab has a `data-tooltip` naming it plus one line on what is inside, and its label in `.ncm-admin-tab__lb`. When the row overflows, `AdminPanelApp._wireTabFold` (ResizeObserver, re-wired each render) folds labels in a fixed order — other groups' tabs first, then the rest of the open group; the open tab always keeps its label; counts ride the folded icon. All nine fit at 820px today. Number keys follow the bar's order (`AdminPanelApp.TAB_KEYS`: 1 overview · 2 messages · 3 contacts · 4 spam · 5 networks · 6 security · 7 terminals · 8 shards · 9 tools — Security had no key before). A new tab goes into its group in admin-panel.hbs and TAB_KEYS.
- **A tab's `onRender` is wrapped in try/catch by `AdminPanelApp._onRender`** (2026-10-29): it runs BEFORE the shared steps (tab-bar label folding, `_tagAddresses`, hotbar drag handles), and an exception there left the whole panel half-styled — the dev saw it switching Players → Profile to At a glance (addresses changed colour, the tab bar showed every label). Cause was `_wireKnown` on At a glance's list-less `.ncm-ct2-kn` section; it now returns when there is no list.
- **The header/HUD/footer of the panel shell are gone** — the tab bar is the only chrome (a keyboard-shortcut hint sits at its right end as a tooltip); default height 760.
- **The colour hierarchy (dev-approved mock `dev/mockups/admin-color-hierarchy-mock.html`)** — the admin presets in `themes/admin-theme.css` define a background ladder (`--ncm-bg-canvas` → `-pane` (list) → `-pane-alt` (detail) → `-card` → `-card-head` (bands) → `-hover` → `-raised`), text tiers + roles (`--ncm-text-heading` / primary(body) / `-label` / `-hint`, and `--ncm-text-addr` (emails, soft blue mono) / `-time` / `-num`), and ONE meaning per colour (`--ncm-act` click/selected · `--ncm-attn` needs you · `--ncm-bad` wrong · `--ncm-good` fine · `--ncm-money` eddies ONLY · `--ncm-secret` anonymous/aliases/encrypted). The old names map onto them (`--ncm-secondary` = act, **`--ncm-accent` = attn — money is `--ncm-money`, not accent**, `--ncm-danger`/`--ncm-primary` = bad, `--ncm-bg-deep/base/mid/surface/elevated` = canvas/pane/pane-alt/card-head/raised). **`styles/components/admin-panel/admin-hierarchy.css`** (imported last from admin-panel.css) applies it across every redesigned tab: panes lifted off the canvas, group headers + section heads as bands, selected = cyan tint + 4px edge, unread = amber edge, flags as filled pills by meaning, decoration icons neutral, names bold heading tone, times mono. **`AdminPanelApp._tagAddresses()`** (every render) wraps each email address in the tab content in `<span class="ncm-addr">` so it wears the address role. New admin UI: use the meaning tokens, never a colour for decoration.
- **Page heads (2026-10-28)** — every tab and sub-tab opens with the shared `<header class="ncm-tabhd"><i class="fas …"></i><div><h2>Title</h2><p>One plain sentence.</p></div></header>` (admin-hierarchy.css; ONE slim line — icon · TITLE · description with an ellipsis, full text on hover via a data-tooltip AdminPanelApp sets each render; wraps only in a pop-out; optional `.ncm-tabhd__end`), under the sub-tab nav. Messages' old `ncm-msg2-hd`, Security's `ncm-sec-admin-viewhd` and Networks' `ncm-net2-viewhd` are gone. A new admin view needs one.
- **Tag pills — every marker in a detail header is ONE shape, `.ncm-tagp`** (admin-hierarchy.css): its colour is `--tc`, set either by a meaning class (`is-act` / `is-attn` (= `is-warn`) / `is-bad` / `is-good` / `is-money` / `is-secret`, plus `is-plain` neutral and `is-time` a dim mono stamp) or by a **category hue picked from the tag's NAME** — `style="--tc:{{ncm-hue name}}"`, the helper in `scripts/utils/tagHue.js` (djb2 hash → `--ncm-hue-1..8`, defined per admin preset, registered in registerTemplates.js; the sims and the four admin previews register it too), so the same organisation / role / scene / holder reads the same colour on every row. Hues say *which one*, never *what is wrong*: a state is a meaning (shard status Locked/Black ICE = bad, Breached = good; a terminal classification matching secret/black/eyes = bad, restricted/confidential = attn), never a hue. Used on: Contacts detail (identity tags, role/org/place/last-mail meta; players: played by / unread / last mail; groups: member count in the group colour), Terminals (status, classification, account Admin/Hidden/Private, the owner/org/location/carried-by line now `sel.where[]`), Shards (status, holder, preset, folder, maker, editable, hardened, network, tethered), Networks (scenes, ways in, effect tags — Anonymous is `secret`, users). **Detail-header actions sit UNDER the text** (Contacts dhead now holds `.ncm-ct2-acts` in column 2; Terminals moves `__acts` to row 2 and re-anchors the ⋯ menu) — in the right-hand column they squeezed the tags one per line.
- **The right column (every detail pane) — section bands + typed values** (admin-hierarchy.css). Every detail section heading (`.ncm-ct2-sec > .k`, `.ncm-term2-k`, `.ncm-sh2-sec__k`, `.ncm-net2-k2`, `.ncm-sec-admin-card__hd`) is a full-bleed band with a 3px edge + icon in the section's tone `--sk`, picked from its HEADING ICON with `:has()` (bolt/warning = attn · lock/shield/mask = secret · skull/ban = bad · coins = money · handshake = good · location/map = hue-3 · sliders/gear = hue-7 · file/folder/inbox/microchip = hue-2 · scroll/clock/id-card/note = label · else act) — so a new section gets a tone by choosing its icon, with no template change. Key/value rows: key = `--ncm-text-label`, value = heading tone 600, a dashed separator; a value that NAMES something carries `style="--tc:{{ncm-hue name}}"` and renders in that hue with a small square before it (terminal Setup: uplink, reach, pinned, preset; contact Details: character, folder; tags become pills); a state value uses `.ncm-kvv.is-good|bad|attn|secret`, a phone `.is-mono`, a blank `.is-dim` (hint, italic). Contacts `kv` rows are `{l, v, cls, hue, tags}` built in `ContactsTab._detailContext`.
- **Per-tab colour pass (2026-10-27, every tab):** each tab's additions sit in a marked "colour hierarchy pass" block at the end of its own CSS. The agreed readings, keep them consistent: **a lock / secured / hidden is violet (`secret`), never red** — red is reserved for Black ICE, bricked, failed, lockouts, burned, dead zones; **unread / waiting is amber**, never red; a count chip FILLS only when it needs you (amber) or is bad (red), a descriptive count only colours its number; trust is one scale (suspicious red → cautious amber → neutral grey → trusted soft green → implicit green); a network wears ITS OWN configured colour (pulled toward text on clean-light), categories (spam categories, tile tabs, target kinds, roles, orgs) wear hues; faces carry the person's name hue (`--tc` on `.ncm-msg2-av`). `:where(.ncm-admin-panel .ncm-tagp)` holds the pill's default `--tc` at zero specificity so a tab's own CSS can set it. **The palette is NEON (2026-10-28, dev: the softer set was "too pastel and cutesy, doesn't read as cyberpunk"; mock `dev/mockups/admin-hue-retune-mock.html`):** every hue and meaning colour sits at the max chroma the screen can show at its lightness. The full-strength colour draws what you LOOK at (tag border + icon + a faint glow, the square before a named value, rings, band edges); tag and named-value TEXT keeps **`--ncm-ink`** of it (63% dark · 72% light · 76% high-contrast, set per preset in admin-theme.css) mixed toward the heading tone. Any colour that is also used as plain text was lifted/deepened until it reads ≥ 4.6:1 on every admin background (that is why clean-dark red is `#ff767f`, not a deeper neon). **Red stays meaning-only (bad) and violet stays meaning-only (secret)** — category hues are kept ≥ 25° of hue away from all six meaning colours, which leaves four free families, so the eight category hues share them by lightness (two pinks, three blues, two teals, a lime). Measured with the Terminals `?contrast=1` and Shards `?audit=1` previews: 0 text nodes under 4.5 in all three presets. The Security tab's `.ncm-sec-admin-card * { box-shadow:none }` killed the band edges — re-allowed tab-side. Still old: inline hex in ToolsTab's health-check / time dialogs; tab CSS still uses the old `--ncm-success/warning/danger/purple` names in places (they resolve, but don't carry the new meanings).

## Architecture: Admin → Contacts (the GM Contact Manager folded in)

Mock `dev/mockups/admin-contacts-redesign-mock.html`. `game.nightcity.openGMContacts(select)` is an alias for `openAdminTab('contacts', {select})`. RELATIONSHIP_TYPES / TRUST_LABELS / BUILT_IN_ROLES live in `scripts/utils/contactVocab.js`. `gm-contact-manager.css` stays (it styles the shared contact-form partial + the player detail); its `.ncm-gm-*` window rules are dead.
- Files: `ContactsTab.js` (world reads + context + `onAct`), pure `tabs/contactsModel.js` (filters, who-knows index = ONE identity per player entry — master id, else address, else (an entry with neither) the linked actor for the MAIN identity only (`utils/identities.js` `entryStandsFor`, 2026-10-30 hotfix: matching every identity by the shared character marked each alias as known once the main was shared, and `pushToPlayer` refused to share an alias to someone holding the main; sim `test-alias-known.mjs`), `needsALook`, `buildDirectory`, edit sections, matrix tallies), `tab-contacts.hbs`, `tab-contacts.css` (`ncm-ct2-*`). Context `ct2` + `contactSummary.total` (HUD).
- **Directory** = NPCs only; aliases nest under their main; a hit on any identity brings the group. Group role/org/trust/folder/none (`contactsGroupBy`), sort A–Z / recent mail. Filters All · Mail waiting · Nobody knows · Aliases · Encrypted · System addresses · Needs a look. **The toolbar is ONE row** (2026-10-28): search · a **Show** select (those quick filters, one at a time — `data-ct2-select="show"` → `_filter`) · Mail waiting / Needs a look as amber count buttons when non-zero (`dir.alerts`, click = solo that filter) · Filter (scope picker, label hidden until something is picked) · Group · Sort · an icon fold button. It wraps, never clips. Send as = ONE scope picker in the sub-tab bar beside New on EVERY Contacts sub-tab (`ct2.sendAsPicker`, `_sendAsPickerHtml`; label in caps; 2026-10-28 — the strip row of recent chips is gone): the five most recently used NPCs pinned on top (ids `r:<id>`), everyone else below by role. Detail: fix bars (auto-added NPC sender → "Looks right" writes `reviewed:true`, whitelisted in `updateContact`; duplicate address → Change) · mail waiting + Reply as the identity it reached · Who knows them (share = `pushToPlayer`, take back = remove matching entries from that character's `contacts` flag) · trust · identities · recent mail · details · GM notes (autosave on blur).
- **Default folds (2026-10-28, dev-approved rule)** — Directory groups AND Players → Who they know groups: the first group open, the rest closed; a closed head shows its count plus an amber "N waiting" (Directory also a red needs-a-look count — open heads carry no badge, their rows say it); a search / filter / facet opens everything; a NEW selection opens its group (`_dirSelKey`, until the GM folds it). The GM's own fold/unfold wins over the default: Directory choices are a key → shut|open map persisted per viewer in localStorage `ncm.ct2.dirFolds` (the older `ncm.ct2.dirShut` array is read once as shut choices), Who they know choices are `_knFold` (session, DOM-only — the template stamps `data-dshut` as the fallback the wiring reads; the jump strip records open). Pure rules `contactsModel.foldShut` / `foldBadges` / `readFoldChoices`; the header carries `data-shut` so a click records the opposite of what it shows. Sim in `test-admin-contacts.mjs`.
- **Edit in place** — sections Identity · Addresses & aliases · Security & ICE · Trust · Notes, scroll-spy strip, dirty dots, pinned Save/Cancel, guard on leaving with unsaved edits. Backed by `_draft` (re-renders never lose edits). Save = `_collectForm()` → `addContact`/`updateContact` (also saves the ICE fields the old window dropped: `encryptionSkill`, `maxBreachAttempts`, `iceSource`, `iceCustom*`, `iceActorId`, `revealTrust`). Pop out mid-edit carries the draft (`openTo({edit})`). From an actor: ActorImportPickerApp → `openAdminTab('contacts', {select:{create:'actor', actorId}})`.
- **Players → Edit + Add an alias are themed dialogs (2026-10-29, dev: "needs some design love")** — pure markup `scripts/utils/personDialogs.js` (`playerEditHtml`, `aliasDialogHtml`, `looksLikeAddress`, `initialsOf`), styles `ncm-pe-*` in ncm-dialog.css, wiring `ContactsTab._wirePersonDialog` (picture tile + Browse → FilePicker, the header card repaints as the GM types, a bad address is flagged under its field). Edit shows who plays the character and the aliases as cards (main starred) with Add an alias / Open the character sheet. **Fixed on the way:** the address check was `/^[^s@]+@[^s@]+$/` (a lost backslash) and refused every address containing the letter s; now `looksLikeAddress`.
- **Players → Who they know / Groups they hold** (mock `dev/mockups/admin-players-known-mock.html`): a searchable list grouped by Role (default) / Org / A–Z (`_knBy`, op `knBy`; `_knownContext`), sticky foldable group heads in the role/org hue, rows = name + role/org tags + address + this player's own last mail or "N waiting" (the mail index's `recent` rows now carry `withActor` + `unread`), a hover ✕ that takes the contact back (`knTake` → `_setKnows(…, false)`; only that identity goes — another identity of the same character is a separate contact), and the player's self-added contacts as a dim "Added themselves" group. Search and folds are DOM-only (`_wireKnown`, `_knShut`), never a re-render. Groups they hold = **Pushed by you** (member faces, when pushed, **out of date** when the master group's name/colour/members differ from the copy — content, not `updatedAt`, which moves on bookkeeping saves — with Update = re-push `pGroupUpdate` and ✕ = `pGroupTake` → `deleteActorGroup`) and **Their own** (eye = show member names, `pGroupPeek`). CSS `ncm-ct2-kn__*` / `ncm-ct2-gh__*`. **The MODULES (2026-10-27, mock `dev/mockups/admin-who-they-know-modules-mock.html`, dev: "immersive but clean little interactive modules that fit inside these areas")** sit above that list and narrow it: **Orbit** (SVG; the character in the middle, every contact a dot on a ring by how recently they and this character wrote — this week / this month / longer / never; colour = role hue, size = mail, a dashed amber ring = waiting; click a ring → `knRing`, a dot → `knPick`; a slow radar sweep), **Roles** (an equaliser, one bar per role, amber cap when someone in it is waiting; `knRole`, several at once), **Waiting on you** (pulsing rows → `knPick`), and the **Contact card** for the picked contact (role/org tags, mail count, address, trust bar, last mail, Reply as / Write as / Open / Take back — existing ops). Filters show as removable chips with "N of M" (`knClear`). **The instruments live on their own inner tab** (2026-10-28): a selected player shows **Profile | At a glance** (`pTab`, `_pTab`/`_pTabFor`, resets to Profile on another character) — At a glance = Orbit/Roles/Waiting/Contact card; Profile = the list, Groups they hold, Identities (the filter chips show on both). The old Hide toggle (`knMods`) is gone. Pure rules `contactsModel` `KN_RINGS`, `knRing`, `knPasses`, `knownModules` (deterministic dot positions); state `_knRing/_knRoles/_knSel/_knMods`. Pop-out puts the orbit beside the others. Motion has a reduced-motion veto. CSS `ncm-ct2-km*`. **The contact list is a foldable navigator module** (`.ncm-ct2-knnav`): a jump strip of the groups with counts that **FILTERS** (2026-10-29, dev: "only show the appropriate accordions and hide the other ones"): a chip shows ONLY that group, open, and hides the rest (`_knSolo`, DOM-only, reset for another character); the lit chip again or the leading **All** chip brings every group back; search works inside the narrowed group; the search / Role · Org · A–Z / Share row sits ABOVE the chips; A–Z groups have NO icon (`fa-font` draws a letter A, so every chip read "A B 1"); with nothing narrowed the chip of the group you are scrolled into is marked (`is-here`) and a fold/open-all, above the existing search + Role/Org/A–Z list. **Groups they hold is the GROUP RACK** (option A of `dev/mockups/admin-groups-held-modules-mock.html`): every held group a tile with a status light (sync green · stale blinking amber · their own hollow · your original deleted red), out-of-date first; filter chips with counts (`ghF`, an emptied chip lets go) and a group-or-person search past 6 (DOM-only); your groups they DON'T hold as dashed push tiles; clicking a tile (`ghSel`) opens a tray of members — for a stale copy what changed since the push (`ghDiff`: + new / struck removed) — with Update their copy (`pGroupUpdate`) / Take back (`pGroupTake`) / Push it to them (`ghPush`). Pure `ghState`, `groupRack`, `ghDiff`; context `_groupRackContext`. **Both fold DOM-only** (`[data-fold-mod]` → `_wireFolds`, grid-rows animation, `_ghShut` / `_knNavShut` for the session), like the Orbit's Hide. Sim `test-known-modules.mjs` (43). **Design direction set here (dev): where an area is a long list, prefer small interactive modules over plain list layouts.**
- **Buttons (2026-10-28, dev: "message, inbox, edit… look really muted"):** secondary `.ncm-ct2-btn` = a raised card (`--ncm-bg-card`), full-strength text, full-colour icon; `--primary` = the ONE filled neon action with `--ncm-text-inverse` text and a faint glow. The block at the end of tab-contacts.css uses a doubled class (0,3,1) to beat the older rules. **Harness note:** the panel window is `.ncm-app.ncm-admin-panel` AND contains an inner `div.ncm-admin-panel`, so `.ncm-app .ncm-admin-panel …` rules DO match in Foundry — a test page without that inner div shows those controls unstyled (white tabs) when nothing is wrong.
- **Icons + avatars carry colour** (2026-10-27): every initials avatar in the tab wears its name hue (`style="--tc:{{ncm-hue name}}"` on `.ncm-ct2-av`); Recent mail arrows are in (green, down-left) / out (cyan, up-right) squares (`.ncm-ct2-dir`); secondary button icons are toned by `data-op` (message blue, inbox glacier, edit periwinkle, share/push green, delete red); identity stars amber (main) / dim until hover. **Players → Edit** (`pEdit` → `_editPlayer`: name / address / picture, the directory twin follows an address change) also lists the character's aliases and has **Add an alias** (saves, then opens the alias prompt); the Players identities rows have the same ★ make-main as the Directory. The player Contact Manager's toolbar is ONE row (search · tag filter · grid/list, contact-manager.css tail): one height (30px × text scale), radius and surface for all three; the filter is a joined [ALL | ▾] box and the view toggle a joined [grid | list] box; the tag panel opens LEFTWARD (it sits at the right end — opening right was cropped by the window), flipping right (`.is-flip`, measured in `_onToggleTagDropdown`) only when a narrow window wraps it to the left edge.
- **Directory → an NPC → Who's writing to them — the SWITCHBOARD** (2026-10-28, mock `dev/mockups/admin-npc-orbit-mock.html` version C, dev-approved; A/B kept as records). Sits on the NPC detail's **Correspondents** inner tab (2026-10-28: the detail is **Profile | Correspondents**, `dTab`, `_dTab`/`_dTabFor`, resets to Profile on another contact, badge = waiting count; the switchboard context is built only while Correspondents is open), for the NPC and its aliases as one group. The hub is split into one wedge per identity (sized by mail, min 40°, main centred left, alias violet); every correspondent's line plugs into the wedge it wrote to. **Distance = what the NPC owes**: mail IN newer than the last mail OUT to that person is *waiting* (not the read flag), and a waiting dot is pulled inside the amber "your turn" ring — longer wait = closer; answered people sit in the "even" band; characters who hold the contact but never wrote sit dashed on the rim. Players are solid dots in their role hue mixed toward amber as they wait (brightness = recency), NPCs dim outlines; an arrow on each line says who wrote last (amber = theirs, your turn). Under it the **correspondent card** (the reply queue was removed 2026-10-28; `npcSwitchboard` returns `firstWaiting` / `waitingTotal` / `waitingPeople` instead of `queue`) (identities used, waiting subjects, last 3, trust, Write-as toggle, Reply / Write / Conversation / inbox / Share). Rules are pure: `contactsModel.npcSwitchboard` (+ `sbPeople`, `sbRadius`, `sbWedges`, `sbAnnulus`, `sbAge`); `ContactsTab._switchboardContext` reads only this group's rows out of `_mail` (whose `recent` rows now carry `ident`, `journalId`, `pageId`, `withEmail`, `spam`), names address-only senders from the directory, skips spam and group copies' joined addresses, and computes dot styles with `tagHue`. New ops `sbPick` / `sbIdent` / `sbAs` / `sbReply` (reply to that page AS the identity it reached) / `sbWrite` (Send-As rule: character-linked identity by contact id, contact-only as the contact); reuses `trustPlayer` / `whoToggle` / `recentOpen` / `pInbox`. State resets when another NPC is opened. CSS `ncm-ct2-sb*` (meaning colours only; amber TEXT uses `--sb-attn-text`, a darker-on-light twin — measured ≥ 4.5:1 on clean-dark and clean-light); no sweep. Sim `test-npc-switchboard.mjs` (74, rules + the real tab + a real Handlebars render of the snippet). Not live-tested.
- **Directory → Filter** (mock `dev/mockups/admin-roles-filter-mock.html` frame B, 2026-10-27): a multi scope picker beside Group/Sort (`scopeId('dirfacets')`, `_facets`) over Roles / Organizations / Trust with counts and "No role" / "No organization" rows; ids `role:<id>` / `org:<name>` / `trust:<v>`. Pure `contactsModel.passesFacets` / `facetKey`: OR inside a kind, AND across kinds. Active picks show under the toolbar as removable pills (`facetOff`, `facetClear`) with "N of M" (main identities only). Picks force the groups open like a search.
- **Role Manager** (frame A, `RoleManagerDialog.js`, CSS `styles/components/role-manager.css` `ncm-rmgr-*`): one admin Dialog that **repaints in place** (the old one closed and reopened itself on every click). List = search + All / Built-in / Custom / In use chips + per-role usage counts; editor = name, colour (the admin palette `ROLE_PALETTE` tokens or any hex), searchable icon grid (`ROLE_ICONS`), a preview as a tag + a Directory band, who uses it, delete naming how many lose it. Built-ins (read from `contactVocab.BUILT_IN_ROLES`, no private copy any more) are read-only with **Make a custom copy**. **A custom role's colour now drives its admin tags**: `tagHue('role:<id>')` asks the resolver `setRoleColorResolver` (registered in registerTemplates) and falls back to the name hue; AdminPanelApp re-renders Contacts on `roles:updated` / `contacts:masterUpdated`. civilian's icon in the shared table is `person` (it had drifted from the player side's). Probe `dev/mockups/role-manager-probe.html?t=&sel=&new=1`. Sim `test-dir-facets.mjs` (30). Not live-tested.
- **Players** · **Groups** · **Who knows whom** (contacts × characters; groups fold with per-character n/m tallies that share/take back the group; Nobody/Some/Everyone/Changed; Columns scope picker; sticky name column + header; shift-click range; Undo last).
- **Who knows whom → MAP (default; the table above is one click away via the Map | Table switch, `_mxMode`)** — the Map | Table switch, the Highlight chips (None / Known by 2+ / Known by one, then ONE searchable **Group** ScopePicker — `map.groupPicker`, scope id `maphi`, it sets `_mapHi = 'group:<id>'`; a chip per group did not scale, dev 2026-10-28) and the role colour legend share ONE row (the legend is ONE fixed-size **ROLES n ▾** button at the row's right end, `.ncm-ct2-mapkey`; clicking it opens a small list UNDER it, over the map — each role as the map's own glowing diamond + name + how many contacts on the map have it, `roles[].n`. It never changes the row's width: the first cut grew in place, wrapped and jumped to the left (dev). Closed by default, remembered for the session, outside click / Esc close — `wireMapKey`. Its button colours are `!important`: in live Foundry CPR's button background won and it read grey-on-white on clean-dark, which no probe showed) (`.ncm-ct2-mode--one`, 2026-10-28; the old in-map `__bar` is gone, the Table hint shows in its place in table mode) — the net map from the dev-approved mock frame 2A-cyber-grid (`dev/mockups/admin-contacts-modules-mock.html`). Pure rules `tabs/contactsMapModel.js` (layout: characters on an ellipse, a contact between the characters who know them, deterministic; every node snapped to a 10px grid; traces 45° out of the contact then straight in, every bend on a grid intersection; traces on one side of a chip take adjacent grid lines, past `BUS_MAX` (6) they merge into one thick BUS with a ×N badge; name backplates placed off nodes, chips, plates, other names AND lit traces, leader line when moved). Context + DOM wiring `tabs/contactsMap.js` (`buildMapContext`: the real who-knows index, `getPlayerCharacters`, master groups as highlight chips, trust per character, the mail index; hover is DOM-only, click = `mapPick` re-render; **navigation (2026-10-29, dev: "zoom in and out and drag and move and navigate")**, all DOM-only per map uid: − / Fit / + step through `MAP_ZOOMS` (1–4), the WHEEL zooms smoothly about the cursor (`anchorScroll`; shift+wheel still scrolls sideways), DRAG anywhere pans (a drag never counts as a click — the ending click is swallowed in capture, so it never picks a node), DOUBLE-CLICK on empty map zooms in there, and with the map focused arrows pan / + − zoom / 0 fits; grab / grabbing cursors; pure `clampZoom` / `stepZoom` / `anchorScroll`). Partial `templates/admin-panel/partials/contacts-map.hbs` (`TEMPLATES.ADMIN_CONTACTS_MAP`), CSS `admin-panel/contacts-map.css` (`ncm-ct2-map*` / `ncm-ct2-mapp*` / `ncm-ct2-mode*`). The map sits in its OWN scroller (`.ncm-ct2-map__scroll`, max-height min(70vh, 640px)) and the whole view scrolls when the panel is short; the info panel (contact: who knows them with share / take back = `whoToggle`, address, groups, last mail, Reply as / Write as / Open / Share with the rest = `mapShareAll`; character: player, only-them, groups held, group members missing → `pGroupUpdate`, inbox / write / contacts; nothing picked: a legend) slides in on a NEW pick only. Contacts nobody knows sit in the dock under the map. Sim `test-contacts-map.mjs` (74); probe `dev/mockups/contacts-map-probe.html?sel=&hi=&t=&w=&h=&z=` (file:// + --allow-file-access-from-files — the mockup server has no Handlebars route). Not live-tested.
- **Every Take back confirms** (2026-10-28, dev): `ContactsTab._confirmTakeBack(names, actorId, {what, note})` (an `adminConfirm` naming what and from whom, list shortened past 6) guards Who knows them / the map panel (`whoToggle` when it would remove), `knTake`, a matrix cell or shift-click range that removes, a group tally that removes, and taking a pushed group back (`gToggle`; `pGroupTake` already confirmed). Sharing and Undo never ask. `test-admin-contacts` asserts No keeps the contact.
- **Voice field (2026-10-28, dev-approved)** — `contact.voice` ("how this NPC talks"), a GM-only master-contact string beside `notes`: whitelisted in `addContact`/`updateContact`, **never on a player copy** (`pushToPlayer` blanks it like `notes`; `ContactRepository._normalizeContact` has no such field), export/import carry it. **An alias inherits its main identity's voice unless it has its own** — the pure `voiceFor` / `voiceInfo(contacts, contact)` in `utils/identities.js` (a contact-only NPC's alias inherits the root's); `ContactsTab._voiceContext` feeds BOTH the detail pane (the GM-notes section is now a two-box `.ncm-ct2-gmnotes` grid: Notes + Voice, `[data-ct2-voice]` autosaves on blur via `_saveVoice`, an inherited voice is the dimmed PLACEHOLDER with "from <main>" so typing over it is the override and clearing lets the main's through) and the edit form's Notes section (`name="voice"`, reached via `@root.ct2.dir.edit.voice` — never `../` inside an `{{#if}}`; `_edSetValue('voice')` dirties `notes`). The shared contact-form partial gains it inside the GM-only fieldset only; the player Contact Manager never reads it. Sims: `test-identities` (85), `test-admin-contacts` (185).
- **Players + Groups wear the Directory's ONE-ROW toolbar too (2026-10-28, dev: "a lot of players or groups… the same idea from directory")** — `.ncm-ct2-tb--one` reused: Players = search (character · player · address · the user's viewed scene) · **Show** (`pShow`: All · Has unread · No address · Online · Offline · Holds a group) · an amber "unread" sum + a red "no address" count (`pFilter`, solo) · **Group** (`pGroup`: None · Player · Scene · Online, `.ncm-ct2-grp` fold heads with the Directory's `foldShut` rule, `pFold`/`pFoldAll`, remembered in `ncm.ct2.playerFolds`) · **Sort** (`pSort`: A–Z · Unread · Last mail · Player); Groups = search (group OR a member's name/address) · **Show** (`gShow`: All · Pushed · Not pushed · Out of date · Never used) · an amber out-of-date count (`gFilter`) · **Sort** (`gSort`: A–Z · Recently used · Size · Pushed to most), no Group select, and past `GROUPS_RAIL_LIMIT` (12) the rail folds behind "+N more" (`gMore`; the selected group is always shown; a search/filter opens all). Both print "Showing N of M" when narrowed. State `_pSearch/_pShow/_pGroup/_pSort`, `_gSearch/_gShow/_gSort/_gMore`; the search boxes are `data-ct2-q="players"|"groups"` on the same debounced listener; the selects are `_onSelect` branches. Pure rules in contactsModel: `passesPlayerFilter` / `sortPlayers` / `groupPlayers` / `playerHaystack`, `passesGroupFilter` / `sortGroups` / `groupHaystack` / `foldTail`, and **`groupSig` is the ONE "out of date" signature** (the Players rack reads it too). `_pcs()` now carries `online` / `sceneName` (the Overview's `user.active` + `user.viewedScene` rule). Not live-tested.
- `openTo`: contact id · `{create:'blank'|'actor', actorId}` · `{edit}` · `{view, actorId, groupId}`. Not placed: batch tag, the tag registry, the old window's keyboard shortcuts. Sim `test-admin-contacts.mjs` (260).

## Architecture: Admin → Networks (the Network Manager folded in)

Mock `dev/mockups/admin-networks-redesign-mock.html`. Every Network Manager opener (`openNetworkManagement` / `openNetworkManager` / `openNetworkManagerToNetwork(id)` / `…ToLogs` / `…ToCreate`, the scene-control button, the macro API, CreateNetworkDialog's "open in manager") → `openAdminTab('networks', { popout: true, select })`. `openTo`: `'<id>'` edit · `'show:<id>'` · `'log:<id>'` · `'view:scene|all|scenes|log'` · `'new'`.
