Composer: **doc-type + classification are datalist comboboxes** (pick common values or type your own; classification drives the stamp color); the Dated field is a `.ncm-doc-datechip` picker; a **signature-image** FilePicker row; and the body is a **custom block engine** (dropped the generic list engine): `_docBlockHtml`/`_collectDocBlocks` inject blocks, three add buttons (Paragraph/List/Figure), text blocks are `contenteditable` (`data-doc-text`) with a per-block hover toolbar — `docFmt` (bold/italic via `execCommand` on the focused block; `.ncm-docpage` mousedown-preventDefault preserves the selection) + `docToggleQuote`/`docToggleRedact`/`docToggleOrdered` (class-toggle, `.is-active`) + `docBrowseFigure`. Verified: `test-doc.mjs` 18/18 (real enricher+partial: text/list/figure/quote/redact + inline HTML + date + classColor + signature image + legacy) + headless `doc-render.png`.
- `chatlog` — IM transcript (redesign 2026-07-12): app/channel header + participant chips, colored sender labels (auto-cycle `CT_COLORS`), the `me` participant's bubbles sit **right**, same-sender grouping (name on first), `deleted` → tombstone. **Message kinds** (`kind`): `text` (default) · `photo` (`image`+`caption` → photo bubble, `galleryView` lightbox) · `system` (centered notice, `system` text + `sysIcon`, no sender). **Dates**: each message has a free diegetic `time` string PLUS an optional ISO `date` — the enricher emits a **day separator** (`daySep`) whenever the day changes, so the feed groups by day. **Reactions**: `reactions: [emoji]` → aggregated to `[{emoji,count}]` tags under the bubble. **Seen**: `seen:true` (+`seenTime`) renders a "✓✓ Seen" receipt after a bubble. Back-compat: no kind=text, no date=no separators. **Composer = the texting-app editor, upgraded** (`_chatBubbleHtml` now stores the WHOLE message in `data-*` attrs; custom `_collectChat()` walks them — dropped the generic list engine + named `chatMsgs__*` inputs): **sender quick-pick chips** (`_syncChatSenderChips` → `chatPickSender` fills the sender; each chip's ★ `chatSetOwner` sets the device owner = `chatMe`, bubbles right), a **type toggle** (`chatType` text/photo/system → swaps the bar inputs + system-preset dropdown `CHAT_SYS_PRESETS`), a **sticky date chip** (CyberTimePicker → `chatDate`, `_paintChatDate`; sticks across sends), an **emoji palette** (`CHAT_EMOJI` ~32 curated, `chatEmoji`/`chatEmojiPick` insert at caret), and per-bubble **reaction add** (`chatReactAdd` → palette in react mode) + **Seen toggle** (`chatSeen`) + tombstone. Photo type gets a FilePicker (`chatBrowsePhoto`). **Compose-area cleanup (2026-07-12):** the compose zone is three tidy rows (`.ncm-chatedit__compose`) — a **toolbar** (segmented Type control `.ncm-chat-seg` + one **When** picker), a **sender chips** row (`data-chat-srow`, hidden for system), and an **input row** (emoji · attach/preset · message · Send). **Time + date are ONE control**: the **When** chip (`[name="chatWhen"]`, the shared `openTimePicker` targeting it) sets a full ISO; `_chatWhen()` derives `date`=ISO-date (grouping) + `time`=`formatCyberDate(iso,{timeOnly})` (bubble display) — no separate time field, no free-typed time. It **sticks** across sends; editing a bubble borrows the field then `_chatEditDone` restores the sticky value (`_chatStickyWhen`); legacy messages (date+time, no ISO) reconstruct via `_reconWhen`. Bubbles also store `data-when`; `_collectChat` emits `when` (enricher still uses date+time, unchanged). The old separate "Device owner" text field is gone (owner is chip-★ only); sender is chip-only with an inline `+ new` input (`chatNewSender` reveals it). Photo mode → an **Attach chip** (`_chatSetAttachLabel` shows the filename); system mode hides the sender row + shows the preset dropdown. Verified: `test-chat.mjs` 20/20 (enricher+partial: kinds, day separators, reactions aggregate, seen, back-compat) + `test-chat-collect.mjs` 13/13 (real `_chatBubbleHtml` data-attrs + `_collectChat` DOM-walk) + headless `chatlog-render.png` (viewer) & `chat-editor2.png` (compose area; mocks `dev/mockups/chatlog-redesign-mock.html` + `chatlog-compose-area-mock.html`).
- `record` — searchable subject **database** (§8c "look someone up → intel"; **typed-field redesign 2026-07-12**). A record = a subject (`{ name, status?, statusColor?, flagged?, portrait? }`) + a flexible **`fields: [{ kind, value, label? }]`** list drawn from **`RECORD_FIELD_KINDS`** (ContentRenderers, exported) — core (phone/alias/address/date/id/note/threat) + security (clearance/priors/bounty/warrant/armed) + work (org/role/dept/empId/grade) + medical (blood/allergies/cyberware/condition/nextofkin/trauma) + asset (vehicle/plate/vin/owner/reg) + power (image=evidence→lightbox, rating=1–5 stars) + custom(kv). Each kind has `{label, icon(FA), type, group, full?, unit?}`; `type` drives render/authoring (text·number+unit·date(CyberTimePicker)·threat(1–4 meter)·rating(1–5 stars)·flag(boolean chip, false=hidden)·image·textarea(note,full-width)·customkv). **`recordType`** is a **`RECORD_DB_KINDS`** quick-start key (personnel/criminal/employee/medical/vehicle/contacts → a `seed` field list for new cards). The enricher normalizes each record → portrait/initials, idLabel (an `id`-kind field pulled out, else top-level id), status pill, and a display-field list (meter/stars/flag chip/evidence thumb/date label). **Back-compat: legacy flat records** (`role/org/address/note` top-level) migrate to synthetic fields via `RECORD_LEGACY_MAP` — old data renders unchanged. The **search box is a display filter** (document-level delegated `input` on `[data-db-search]`, one listener for all viewers + composer preview, `typeof document`-guarded) and indexes every field value. **Composer = a card-builder** (NOT the generic list engine — fields vary per record): `_recordCardHtml`/`_recFieldHtml`/`_recPaletteHtml` inject full subject cards (portrait FilePicker tile, name, status-cycle pill, flag, typed field rows, a grouped **+field palette**), a DB-kind quick-start bar (`recKind` seeds new cards), and `_collectRecords()` walks the DOM (date fields carry a `foundry.utils.randomID`-named hidden input so the shared CyberTimePicker can target them; the spreadsheet-grid DOM-walk precedent). Actions: recAddSubject/recRemoveSubject/recAddField/recRemoveField/recStatusCycle/recFlag/recPip/recFlagField/recPortrait/recKind. **Every field renders a distinct label** (muted uppercase fixed-width key column vs a brighter `text-primary` value — the "PHONE 1111 blends" fix); flag fields show the label as a red chip, a `false` flag is hidden. Cards are **collapsible** (`recToggleCard` → `.is-collapsed` hides fields+palette, shows an "N fields" summary — close the ones you're done with) and **duplicatable** (`recDuplicate` → `_collectOneRecord` clones the card into a new expanded "… (copy)" card). **Date fields carry an editable label** (Last seen / Intake / DOB — the default "Date" is never what you mean; stored as `field.label`, read generically from any `.ncm-recfield__key` in `_collectOneRecord`). **CSV import/export** (`recExportCsv`/`recImportCsv`): export writes `name,status,flagged` + one column per distinct field keyed on `kind+label` (labeled dates → distinct columns, header = the field's display label); import reuses the spreadsheet `_parseCsv`, resolves each header via `_recCsvResolveHeader` (name/status/flagged special → kind key/label → else a **custom** field), coerces values by kind (`_recCsvParseVal`: flag y/n, number strips units, date→ISO), and **replaces** the card list. CSV caveat: a custom-labeled date (e.g. "Last seen") re-imports as a custom TEXT field (still readable; the date-picker affordance is the one thing lost) — name a date column `Date` to keep it a date. Verified: `test-records.mjs` 27/27 (enricher+partial, new+legacy, portraits) + `test-rec-csv.mjs` 25/25 (real header/value/cell helpers + full round-trip via `_parseCsv`) + headless `records-render.png` (viewer) & `rec-editor.png` (card builder, collapsed, CSV row).
- `schematic` — annotated **blueprint** (heist intel): grid-frame stage, numbered pins absolutely positioned at authored `x`/`y` **percent** coords (clamped 0–100 in the enricher), pin hover = native tooltip, legend below, image click reuses the `galleryView` lightbox, `linkedSceneId` → GM "Go to Scene" (the LOCATION gating). **Redesign (#33, 2026-07-12):** annotations are now **typed markers** — `SCHEM_MARKERS` (ContentRenderers.js: entry/exit/door/window/guard/camera/alarm/hazard/objective/loot/terminal/pin) maps each `type` → `{label, icon, color, group}` (`schemMarkerInfo(type)` fallback = pin). The enricher stamps per-pin `icon`/`color`/`markerLabel`/`group`/`idx`, resolves **`routes: [{from,to,color?}]`** (pin ids) into `{x1,y1,x2,y2,color}` line coords (orphans dropped), groups the legend via `legendGroups` (SCHEM_GROUP_ORDER + a 'Locked' bucket), and applies **fog** to `locked && !isGM` pins (icon→`fa-question`, label/note/sceneId stripped, `isFog:true`). A **per-marker `sceneId`** adds a per-pin GM "Go to Scene" button (distinct from the whole-map `linkedSceneId`). Locked markers → a **SCOUT** roll-to-reveal (`content-body.hbs` emits `contentUnlock data-unlock-kind="schematic" data-index="{idx}"`; `SiphonFlow.runContentUnlock` schematic branch flips that annotation's `locked` by index via `persistContentData`, per-marker `unlockSkill`/`unlockDV` beat the vault-level pair). **Composer = an interactive stage** (`DataShardComposer._wireSchemStage`, replaced the old `_syncSchemStage`/pin-row list + `SCHEM_PIN_FIELDS`): a **marker palette** (`_schemPaletteHtml`, Select · Route · one chip per type grouped) sets `this._schemTool`; click the plan to drop a typed pin, click a pin to open the **inline edit popover** (label/note/type/color/scene/lock + per-pin unlock skill+DV, `_schemOpenPop`/`_schemPopInput`), **drag** to move (`_schemPointerDown`), **Route** mode links two pins into `this._schemRoutes`. The stage DOM is the source of truth — pins are `.ncm-schem-pin` spans with data-attrs, routes render into the `[data-schem-routes]` SVG, and `_collectSchematic()` walks both into `{annotations, routes}` (drops empty optional fields + orphan routes). CSS: composer stage/palette/pins/popover in item-inbox.css, viewer markers/routes/fog/legend-groups in content-types.css. **Legend legibility pass + GM lock toggles (2026-07-13, #35c):** legend rows are **card rows** (bg + border + `--ct-c` left edge, `.ncm-ct-schem__legtx` STACKS the 13px display-font label over an 11px body-font note — the old inline 10.5px mono was unreadable under the plan), and the **GM gets a per-point lock toggle** in each row (`.ncm-ct-schem__lock`, action `schemLockToggle` → `SiphonFlow.toggleSchematicLock`, GM-guarded) to flip a marker's fog live at the table without the composer; players still SCOUT-roll.
- `payload` — **utility PROGRAM** the operator runs (overhaul 2026-07-12; the utility sibling of the hostile `malware`). Hybrid **manifest → console → active** card: a signed-package header (name/publisher/version/size/charges + effect pill) + metadata grid + authored **requirements** checklist → **RUN** executes into an authored **run-log console** (`output[]` lines, severity-colored) → a timed effect (`effect.duration > 0`) shows a green **RUNNING** strip with a `timeService` **countdown + KILL**; done/expired/killed footers otherwise. **Executing applies a real module-native effect** (`effect.type`): **icebreaker** (unseal every sealed sibling entry in the host journal — gallery/avlog/creds/declassify), **skeletonKey** (clear the host's SecurityService lockouts), **scanner** (report hidden accounts / off-net files, written to `scanReport`), **jammer** (drop the terminal uplink → LOCAL ONLY; restored on expiry/kill), **adjudicate** (mark-run + GM alert, the always-works fallback) — plus an optional **linked world Macro** (`effect.macroId`, GM-run). **Execute flow = `SiphonFlow.runPayloadExecute`**: the gate roll runs client-side (SecurityConsole, optional `executeRequire`/`executeSkill`/`executeDV`, `traceOnFail`), but the effect + payload state write are **GM-owned** — a GM applies directly (`applyPayloadEffect`), a player relays the roll OUTCOME via `SOCKET_OPS.PAYLOAD_EFFECT_REQUEST` (ONE writer, no races; the malware/siphon split). `runPayloadKill` ends a timed effect early (+ reverses jammer); a GM reaper (`reapExpiredPayloads`, registerTerminalSystem ready/66, 60s) flips expired effects to done + restores reversible ones. **Disguise (trojan)** is IMPLEMENTED: `disguised` + `disguiseType` → `enrichContent` swaps a player's view to the **cover type** (name/description mapped into a note/message/eddies/dossier via `_disguiseCover`; the real payload never reaches their DOM), while the GM sees the real payload + a red `DISGUISED AS X` tag + a **REVEAL** button (`payloadSniff` force) — `runPayloadSniff` flips `sniffed` so players then see the truth (executing reveals too). Actions `executePayload`/`payloadKill`/`payloadSniff` wired in all three viewers. Composer: a full manifest form + effect/macro/duration selects + `plReqs` requirements list + a **WYSIWYG run-log console editor** (`plOutput`, severity-cycle + Enter=next line, the syslog builder pattern) + execute-gate + disguise rows. **Legacy migration:** old `payloadType`/unknown `effect.type` read fine (unknown effect → `adjudicate`, `payloadType` → `program` label). Verified: `test-payload.mjs` 29/29 (real enricher + real partial, all states + disguise GM/player + legacy) + headless `payload-render.png`.
- `eddies` — a **cred transfer** (redesign 2026-07-14). Cyberpunk RED money lives on cred sticks, so a dead-drop reads as a physical **eb-chip** in the gold-FUI family (corner brackets + `CRED TRANSFER` topbar, like the SecurityConsole/BankHeist) — amount · **SOURCE** (the entry's `from`, enricher reads `entry.from`) · optional signed note · **CLAIM — AUTHORIZE TRANSFER**. The claim re-renders the entry with `claimed:true`; a **`justClaimed`** window (**`claimedRealMs`** within ~12s) stamps `.is-fresh`, and the transfer animation plays **once**: the amount **drains off the chip** while a green ledger line **counts up**, over a deliberately slow (~4.2s) `◈ AUTHORIZING… → ◈ ENCRYPTED HANDSHAKE → ◈ TRANSFER → ✓ FUNDS RELEASED` sequence (a spinner clears top-right; the card holds GOLD via `.is-transferring` then flips green). Beat durations are named consts (`T_AUTH`/`T_HAND`/`T_COUNT`/`T_SETTLE`) at the top of `playEddiesTransfer` — tune there. **It's JS-driven** (`playEddiesTransfer` in ContentRenderers — the count tween), wired via a document-level **`animationstart`** listener that fires when the `.is-fresh` card's trivial `ncm-ct-eddies-arm` keyframe mounts — the zero-per-app-wiring pattern (works in all three viewers). **The template renders the END state** (green / released / full ledger) so a claimed card is always correct even if the animator never runs; `playEddiesTransfer` resets to the start (0 / AUTHORIZING / gold) then counts up. Reduced motion jumps straight to the end. **THE render-remount trap (fixed 2026-07-14 — first the "goes almost instantly after Claim" bug, then the "renders twice" bug):** the viewer re-renders SEVERAL times during one claim (the persist-hook `_debouncedRender` + the claim handler's own `render()`) and each render REPLACES the card element with a fresh one drawn in its green END state (the template's robust default). A captured element reference dies mid-animation; a naïve `_eddiesAnimated`-once guard treated the replacement's re-arm as "already played" and snapped to end (→ "instant"); and an async/await version that only re-painted DURING the count left the remounted card showing green END through the AUTHORIZING/HANDSHAKE waits (→ a visible "renders twice" green flash). Fix — the animation is **ONE time-based rAF loop** (`playEddiesTransfer`): every frame it resolves the LIVE card by entry id (`liveCard()` re-queries `.ncm-ct-eddies[data-eddies-id="…"]`) and REPAINTS the entire state (class + status + amount + deposit) for the current elapsed time via a single `paint(amt, dep, status, gold)`. So a remount at ANY phase self-corrects within one frame — no orphaned element, no idle-wait green flash. A **two-Set guard** (`_eddiesAnimating` in-flight vs `_eddiesAnimated` done) means a re-render's re-arm never starts a SECOND loop, and only a genuine sighting AFTER completion snaps to end. (The claim handlers still do their own `render()` on success — harmless now that the loop is remount-proof; the popup `runSecurityConsole` confirm does NOT cause an app render, it's a plain DOM overlay.) Sim: scratchpad `test-eddies-rerender.mjs` (18/18 — scripts remounts during AUTHORIZING AND during the count through the real single-loop logic). **Gotcha (fixed 2026-07-14):** the window MUST key on a REAL-time stamp — `claimedAt` is GAME time (the in-fiction claim moment), and comparing that to `Date.now()` never matches in a world-time/manual/disguised clock, so the animation silently never played. `runEddiesClaim` + `DataShardService.claimEddies` both persist `claimedRealMs: Date.now()` alongside `claimedAt`; the enricher reads `claimedRealMs`. (`location.justRevealed` sidesteps this by stamping `revealedAt` with real `new Date()`.): a beam sweep, the chip + amount flip green, and a `+N eb → <name>'s ledger` line rises (all CSS, reduced-motion-gated, `--ncm-accent`-keyed so money stays gold under any terminal preset). Claim LOGIC is unchanged (`runEddiesClaim`, still ungated-confirm or optional roll). **Replaced the flat legacy `.ncm-eddies-*` card** (was in item-inbox.css on `--sp-*`); the new `.ncm-ct-eddies*` lives in content-types.css on `--ncm-*` tokens, so it themes in all three viewers. The shard viewer's older bespoke `_showTransitionSplash` claim path still fires its own wire-transfer overlay on top (belt-and-suspenders there only).
- `dossier` — FUI **target file** (redesign 2026-07-13, #35): red corner brackets + `TARGET FILE // NC-DB-xxxx` topbar (fileRef derived from the messageId tail), scan-swept portrait, classification stamp, **threat = a 4-seg meter** (LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH/EXTREME strings still map). **Masked identity** (`faceLocked` + `faceUnlockSkill/DV`, `faceTraceOnFail`): the portrait src AND real name are enricher-stripped from a player's DOM (static-masked silhouette, name → `██████`, alias stays — the composer also swaps the entry **subject** to the alias so the index can't leak); one DECRYPT roll (kind `dossier-face`) unmasks, and the GM sees the face + a **force-REVEAL chip** (`data-unlock-force="1"`, honored by all three viewers' contentUnlock handlers: `force = isGM && (!actor || unlockForce)`). **COMBAT PROFILE** (`profile: {enabled, title?, visibility 'open'|'encrypted'|'gm', skill, dv, stats[], skills[], decrypted}`): a **live read of the linked world actor** — stat values from `actor.system.stats`, skill totals via `SkillService.getAvailableSkills`, NEVER stored (edit the sheet, the dossier updates); `encrypted` keeps the VALUES out of the player DOM (`◈` glow-blanks) until a `dossier-profile` roll, `gm` hides the panel entirely, and the GM gets an open-sheet chip (`[data-doss-sheet]`, document-delegated — no per-app wiring). **Composer = the file itself**: portrait tile IS the FilePicker (linked-actor pick repaints it), the classification input stamps the tile live, threat/security-style click-meter, sections are paper blocks whose REDACT toggle draws bars over the text live (hidden `dossierSectionRedacted-N` checkboxes stay the save contract), profile stat toggle-chips show the actor's live values as badges and skills add as chips from a linked-actor datalist.
- `avlog` — **media deck** (redesign 2026-07-13, #35): a real media URL renders the normal player inside deck chrome (REC chip + source + duration); **no media + a transcript + a parseable `duration` ("MM:SS"/"H:MM:SS") = ▶ TRANSCRIPT PLAYBACK** (the karaoke feed) — per-line start seconds are weighted by text length in the enricher (`data-av-t`, total on `data-av-dur`) and a **document-delegated `[data-av-act]` driver** in ContentRenderers plays it: waveform animates, timecode ticks, played lines dim, the current line glows, pause/replay; real-time `setInterval` is deliberate (UI animation), reduced-motion jumps to the readable end state. Speakers auto-color `c1–c4` by first appearance. **Composer**: the URL input live-previews the REAL player (`resolveMediaEmbed`, type auto-detected into the hidden `avlogMediaType`; `_onBrowseMedia` refreshes explicitly — programmatic sets fire no events), and the transcript is a **feed editor** (speaker quick-chips auto-collected, Enter adds the next line, click a line to edit — ADD becomes UPDATE, hover ✕) whose lines still carry the hidden `transcriptSpeaker/Text-N` inputs (`_collectTranscriptLines` unchanged).
- `location` — **intel postcard** (redesign 2026-07-13, #35; tags CUT + encrypted pin ADDED same day per dev): image backdrop (click → lightbox) with name/district/coords/**HEAT chip** overlaid, an optional **CONTROLLED BY + SECURITY meter strip** (`security` 1–4 → LOW/GUARDED/HIGH/LETHAL), and practical intel rows (`hours/access/approach/contact`) — ALL optional, a legacy location renders a plain postcard. (**Tags were built then removed** — don't reintroduce; a legacy `tags[]` field is silently ignored.) **Encrypted pin** (`locked` + `unlockSkill/unlockDV/traceOnFail`): the ENTIRE pin — image, coordinates, district, description, intel — is enricher-stripped from the DOM (only the name survives); players see a static-scrambled card with scrambled coord glyphs + a DECRYPT button (contentUnlock kind `location`, isCrypt). **The unlock stamps `revealedAt`** and any render within ~12s gets `justRevealed` → the `.is-revealed` **FUI resolve animation** (scan-bar sweep, image de-static via a filter keyframe, coords type-in, staggered strip/intel fade-ins; reduced-motion-gated; real-time `Date.now` is fine here — display-only window). **Composer = the postcard** (type on the card, BROWSE sets the backdrop live, 🎲 rolls flavor coordinates, district datalist) + the raw Scene-ID input is GONE — **Link Scene is the picker Dialog** (calendar precedent) showing the pick as a chip — + an "Encrypted pin" lock row.
- `note` — a **sticky note** (tape strip, slight tilt, authorable paper color, `pinned` thumbtack, `pre-wrap` line breaks). Deliberately trivial — per the doc's rule, pure-flavor content is a NOTE, not a new type.
- `malware` — **quarantined executable**: biohazard file card, payload-type pill, 4-bar threat meter (the creds strength pattern), `deployable` → a shimmer **DEPLOY** button (`deployMalware` action, wired in all three viewers) → `SiphonFlow.runMalwareDeploy`: a normal deploy marks `deployed` + alerts the GM to adjudicate the effect (the payload-execute model); a **`honeypot`** fires the trace instead (`trace-fire` + `showShardTrace` + GM alert + `tripped`). The HONEYPOT tag renders **GM-only** (`{{#if isGM}}`) — players find out by deploying it. Authorable in the composer: an optional **deploy gate** (`deployRequire`/`deploySkill` [SKILL_MAP dropdown]/`deployDV` — a missed roll misfires like a honeypot) and a **linked Black ICE** (`iceActorId`, a `<select>` of world ICE actors using the shard-security filter — `type blackIce`/`black-ice`, `isBlackICE` flag, or "black ice" name). On ANY misfire (honeypot trip or botched gated deploy) the linked ICE attacks the operator via `ICEService.applyDamage` with `{ice:{source:'actor', actorId}}` (ATK + 1d10, the shard hack-fail path); it renders as a second GM-only chip (`.ncm-ct-mal__ice`, skull) next to HONEYPOT, and the ICE actor's **portrait replaces the biohazard file icon for everyone** (flavor art, not a tell — enricher `iceImg`; the icon host needed `overflow:hidden`, the rounded-avatar gotcha).

**Large-dataset scroll:** the spreadsheet table, log feed, ledger, and calendar rail cap height (~300–400px) with a themed `::-webkit-scrollbar` and the spreadsheet's column-letter + header rows are `position:sticky`, so a long file scrolls internally instead of blowing up the window.

**Siphon mechanic (banking):** click **Siphon Funds** → the immersive **Security Console** (see below) picks the amount + rolls the security check inline → on success credit the player's wealth (reusing the eddies/claim wealth-ledger path) + debit the account (`siphoned` flag + balance, GM-relayed since the journal is GM-owned) + lock; on **fail → trace + GM alert**. Amount modes (GM-set): `siphonMode` `cap` (`siphonCap`) | `full` | `percent` (`siphonPercent`).

**Security Console — the shared content-action modal (`scripts/ui/content/SecurityConsole.js` + `styles/components/security-console.css`).** The reusable "security-roll → styled dialog → outcome animation" foundation that replaces vanilla `Dialog` for content actions (proven on siphon; claim/crack/deploy/exfil to follow). `runSecurityConsole(opts)` is a **framework-free promise modal** (NOT an Application) — a themed `.ncm-seccon` panel appended into `opts.host` (the viewer element, so it inherits that viewer's `--ncm-*` preset; falls back to `document.body` = brand default). Features: an optional numeric **amount** input (`{max, unit, label}`), an optional skill-check **gate** (`{require, skill, dv, actor, context, flavor}`) — when `require` is true and an actor is present, the primary button becomes **ATTEMPT**, rolls via `SkillService.performCheck` (posts the chat card, `showChat:true`), and plays an inline pass/fail animation (progress sweep → green success / red fail verdict, the console owns the climax sound `hack-success`/`hack-fail`) before resolving. `require:false`/no gate → a styled confirm, no roll (this is the **"optional per-action, default off"** contract callers read from config). Resolves `{ ok, amount, total, cancelled }`. Accent = `opts.accent` (a `data-ct-color` key → `--ct-c`). Reduced-motion collapses to an instant verdict; ESC/backdrop cancel; Enter attempts. The **stylesheet is `@import`ed after content-types.css** (it depends on `[data-ct-color]{--ct-c}` from there) and carries its own `user-select:text` opt-in. Callers thread `host: this.element` (all three viewers). Verified headless across idle/success/fail states (`seccon.png`).

**All content-action flows route through the console.** `SiphonFlow.js`'s `runSiphon`/`runCrack`/`runEddiesClaim`/`runMalwareDeploy` each take a `host` and open the console: **siphon** (amount + always-gated), **crack** (always-gated; GM force = styled confirm), **claim** (styled confirm; optional gate via `claimRequire`/`claimSkill`/`claimDV`), **deploy** (styled confirm; optional gate via `deployRequire`/`deploySkill`/`deployDV` — a botched gated deploy misfires like a honeypot: trace + `tripped`). "Optional per-action" gates default OFF, so ungated actions are just immersive confirms. Each flow's outcome sounds moved into the console (`hack-success`/`hack-fail` verdict); the flow keeps only its distinct follow-ups (`shard-decrypt` reveal, `trace-fire`, `eddies-claim`). Wired at every call site in all three viewers with `host: this.element`.

**Exfil is a per-action policy (six actions) + the console.** `config.exfil` = `{ agent, shard, forward, leak, sell, wipe }`, each `{ allow, require, skill, dv }` (+ `sell.price`), under the `allowExfil` master. `TerminalService.getExfilPolicy(item)` resolves it with **back-compat**: legacy configs (no `config.exfil`) → agent+shard ON/ungated, the four new actions OFF (today's behavior); master off → all hidden. The viewer builds its exfil menu from the policy (`_exfilMenu` → `EXFIL_META`-ordered chips, gated ones show a lock + DV tooltip); **one dispatcher `_onExfilAction`** runs the console (confirm + the action's optional gate) then routes to `_doExfil{Inbox,Shard,Forward,Leak,Sell,Wipe}`. A **gated FAIL fires a trace and aborts** (logged before the transfer finished). The four new actions: **forward** (→ a chosen contact's Agent inbox as a "Fwd:" message — `TerminalService.exfilForward`, GM direct / player relay `dest:'forward'`), **leak** (a public `ChatMessage` artifact everyone sees — client-side, no relay), **sell** (credits the operator's wealth at `sell.price` via the eddies-ledger path + a GM whisper — client-side), **wipe** (hard-deletes the source page — `TerminalService.exfilWipe`, GM direct / player relay `dest:'wipe'`). The relay extends the existing `SOCKET_OPS.TERMINAL_EXFIL_REQUEST` (`dest` now also `forward`/`wipe`, carrying `forwarder`). Config UI = the Exfil tab's per-action rows (toggle · roll · skill · DV · sell price); the skill is a **`SKILL_MAP` dropdown** (`skillOptions` per row in `_exfilRows`, legacy/hand-typed values kept selectable at the top — the account-editor pattern), as are the composer's banking-siphon and malware-deploy skill fields. Policy resolver logic-verified (13/13) + both surfaces headless-verified (`exfil2.png`). The test macro enables all six (some gated) so they're exercisable.

**Roll-to-unlock (contentUnlock).** `SiphonFlow.runContentUnlock({actor, raw, kind, index, messageId, force, host})` is the shared reveal flow (the crack pattern: always a roll, GM-without-character force-reveals via styled confirm; a miss just fails UNLESS the contentData carries **`traceOnFail: true`** — then `_fireUnlockTrace` plays trace-fire + `showShardTrace` + a client-created **GM whisper** ChatMessage, no socket needed). One action name **`contentUnlock`** (data attrs `data-unlock-kind` + `data-index` + `data-entry-id`), wired in all three viewers (ItemInboxApp static / TerminalViewerApp instance / MessageViewer router → `_unlockTypedContent`). Three shapes: **gallery** — per-image `locked`, DECRYPT reveals ONE frame (per-image `unlockSkill`/`unlockDV` beat the vault-level pair; enricher `effDV` feeds each tooltip); **avlog** — contentData `locked` seals the recording (enricher strips `mediaUrl`/`embedSrc`/`transcript` so nothing leaks in the DOM; "RECORDING SEALED" panel + DECRYPT); **document / dossier / spreadsheet** — `declassify: true` + `declassifySkill`/`declassifyDV` arms a DECLASSIFY button (`.ncm-ct-declass`; spreadsheet gets the `--chip` variant in its top bar) and ONE roll lifts ALL redactions (revealed paragraphs/sections keep `declassified: true` → green `[DECLASSIFIED]` tint; spreadsheet just clears the `redacted` list — the values were always in `rows`); **dossier-face** — `faceLocked` → unmask the dossier identity (`faceUnlockSkill`/`faceUnlockDV`, `faceTraceOnFail`; mutate sets `faceLocked:false, faceRevealed:true`); **dossier-profile** — an `encrypted` combat profile → `profile.decrypted:true` (`profile.skill`/`profile.dv`); **location** — `locked` → decrypt the whole pin (mutate stamps `revealedAt` → the FUI reveal animation). These kinds count as `isCrypt` (purple console, shard-decrypt sound) and the GM REVEAL chip forces via `data-unlock-force="1"`. Related non-roll mutator: **`toggleSchematicLock({raw, index, messageId})`** — the GM's per-point lock checkbox in the schematic legend (action `schemLockToggle`, wired in all three viewers; GM-guarded, flips `annotations[i].locked` via persistContentData). Redacted paragraphs/sections need authored text or declassifying reveals nothing (composer hints say so). All authorable in the composer (lock checkbox + per-frame DV field in the gallery list rows, avlog Sealed row, skill-dropdown/DV/"fail fires a trace" rows per type, shared `_skillOptions`/`_declassCtx` helpers). Verified through the real enrichContent + real partial (scratchpad `test-unlock.mjs`, 27/27 — incl. "locked src/caption/media/transcript NOT in the DOM").

**Entry-header sender resolution (read-time From ↔ Master List).** `ContentRenderers.resolveEntrySender(from)` → `{ name, email, isNode, isDirectory, portrait, initials, contactId, linkedActorId }` (master directory by email; system nodes like `SECCAM-B2` get `isNode`, no email). **Shard viewer**: `_enrichEntry` attaches `enriched.sender` (+ `canAdd`/`canReply`) and `item-inbox.hbs` renders a sender row in the entry header (`.ncm-entry-sender` — avatar-or-initials, name, directory email, and two chips: `addSenderContact` → adds to the viewing player's contacts carrying portrait/actorId/masterContactId, duplicate-safe; `composeToSender` → `game.nightcity.composeMessage({to, subject:'Re: …'})`, shown when `verifyContact(email).verified`). The player-contact email cache is read ONCE per render in `_prepareContext` (async repo → `this._playerContactEmails` Set) so enrichment stays sync — same pattern in `TerminalViewerApp._prepareContext`. **Terminal viewer** already resolved sender name/portrait; fixed: `isContact` now keys on the PLAYER's own list, not the master directory (the old `!!contact` hid Add-to-contacts for every directory sender — exactly the ones worth adding), `_onAddContact` carries the master portrait/actorId/masterContactId + dedupes, and a **Reply via Agent** chip (`terminalReplyAgent`, gated on `canReply`) opens the composer to the sender from the player's own character. **Composer Entry Header UX**: a live status chip under the From input (`_wireFromStatus`, pure DOM, repainted per keystroke): cyan "Linked to master contact X" with portrait / amber "New sender — a master contact will be created on save" / muted "System node — stays as typed" (`.ncm-composer-from-status` in item-inbox.css).

**Shared authorable palette** `CT_COLORS` (green/gold/cyan/red/amber/purple/blue/pink/muted) drives the per-type variety that kills the monochrome: CSS exposes `[data-ct-color="x"]{--ct-c}` (component theming) + `.ncm-ct-c--x`/`.ncm-ct-bg--x` (cell text/bg utilities) + `.ncm-ct-pill`. Subtle motion (staggered fade, blinking prompt, sparkline draw, REUSED pulse, crack shimmer) is `prefers-reduced-motion`-guarded.

**Interactivity (wired 2026-07-02):** the credentials **reveal-eye** (masked dots ↔ plain secret) and the **syslog severity filter chips** (click a severity to solo it; click again or ALL to reset) are client-side-only, wired in BOTH viewers through shared DOM helpers in ContentRenderers (`toggleSecretReveal`/`applySyslogFilter` ← data-actions `credToggleSecret`/`syslogFilter`; secrets render masked by default). **CRACK TO REVEAL** (`SiphonFlow.runCrack`, action `crackCred` carrying `data-entry-id` + `data-cred-index`) rolls `crackSkill` vs `crackDV` — entry-level beats vault-level, defaults Interface / DV 15; a GM with no assigned character force-reveals (anon-trace precedent). The **eddies Claim button is deliberately NOT gated on `interactive`** (like siphon — money is claimable wherever it's found); the terminal implements `claimEddies` via `SiphonFlow.runEddiesClaim`. **All contentData mutations persist through `SiphonFlow.persistContentData`** — GM writes the page directly, players relay via `SOCKET_OPS.CONTENT_UPDATE_REQUEST` (registered in SocketHandlers). That helper also fixed `DataShardService.claimEddies`, whose direct `page.update` **threw for players** (shard journals are OBSERVER) *after* the wealth was already credited — a claim-again exploit — and used the unreliable nested `contentData.*` dot-path write. The **gallery lightbox** (`galleryView` → `openGalleryImage(target, {host})` → the immersive `.ncm-imgv` overlay; ImagePopout survives only as the GM's SHOW PLAYERS broadcast via `shareImage()`) is wired in all three viewers alongside `credToggleSecret`/`syslogFilter`/`deployMalware`. **v12 gotcha (bit us twice — "enlarge won't enlarge" then "Image Enlarger unavailable"): reference `ImagePopout` BARE, not `globalThis.ImagePopout`.** In v12 it's a top-level `class` in a classic script = a global *lexical* binding, so ES modules see it as a bare identifier but `globalThis.ImagePopout` is `undefined` (lexical bindings aren't properties of the global object). The whole codebase already references `Dialog`/`FilePicker`/`Handlebars` bare for the same reason. Use `(typeof ImagePopout !== 'undefined') ? ImagePopout : foundry.applications?.apps?.ImagePopout` (typeof-guarded so an absent binding can't ReferenceError; the namespace is the v13 fallback). Ctor is `new ImagePopout(src, {title, uuid})`. Also read `data-src` via `closest('[data-src]')` since the click may land on the inner `<img>`. Spreadsheet column **sort is not built** (the spreadsheet top-bar chips are status indicators, not buttons). Player-side caveat: after a relayed claim/crack the player's viewer may lag until the journal write syncs back. **Authoring:** the composer now covers **all 18 types** (the macro API remains available for scripted seeding) — most new types are list-engine forms (`GAL_IMG_FIELDS`/`DOC_PARA_FIELDS`/`CHAT_MSG_FIELDS`/`REC_ROW_FIELDS`/`SCHEM_PIN_FIELDS`); the engine gained a `type:'image'` field (text input + per-row FilePicker via `browseListImage`, which dispatches an `input` event so the live preview refreshes), and the chat form's "Device owner" field names the `me` sender (participants auto-derive from messages at collect). The composer's Network Visibility fieldset is hidden for terminal targets (`{{#unless isTerminalTarget}}` — terminal messages live behind the account login, not network gating).

**`interactive` means "this consumer WIRES the content actions" — NOT "is a live viewer".** The terminal AND message viewers passed `interactive: false` while wiring every action, which silently hid the payload **RUN/KILL**, the disguise **REVEAL**, the schematic **SCOUT**, and the GM Go-to-Scene chips in both (the dev's "I've never seen the Run button"). All three viewers now pass `true`; **only the composer's read-only live preview passes `false`**. Corollary: don't add an `interactive` gate to a contentUnlock-style button — gallery/avlog/location/dossier DECRYPTs are deliberately ungated (they must survive any consumer that wires `contentUnlock`).

**The OPERATOR chip renders on the LOGIN screen too, not just the mailbox HUD** (`templates/terminal/partials/operator-chip.hbs`, registered as `TEMPLATES.TERMINAL_OPERATOR_CHIP`; both the hero stat row and the CRT top bar invoke it with the `operator` block from `TerminalViewerApp._operatorChipContext()`, which the HUD now reads too so the two can't drift). Same reason the uplink pill sits there: **the choice matters BEFORE you log in** — a skill-check login rolls against the operator's stats, and finding out you were the wrong PC only afterwards is the bug that paid siphoned eddies to the wrong actor. It reuses the existing `terminalSwitchOperator` action, so it needed no new wiring.

**Operator identity — WHO a viewer acts as.** `resolveOperator()` (helpers.js) is the ONE source for the character every roll, siphon, eddies claim, and skill/stat read uses in the terminal + shard viewers: remembered pick (client setting `operatorActorId`, still owned) → `game.user.character` → the sole owned actor → null. **Never read `game.user.character` directly in a viewer** — that was invisible-and-wrong when a player owns several PCs (siphoned eddies landed on the wrong one). The terminal HUD shows an **OPERATOR chip** (`hud.operator`, `data-action="terminalSwitchOperator"` → `openOperatorPicker(this.element)`, shown only when the user owns >1 actor; a red **NO OPERATOR** chip when there's none) and `_onSwitchOperator` re-resolves the cached `this.actor`/`this.actorId`. `getOperatorActors()` = a **player**'s owned actors; a **GM** gets the world's **player characters** (`hasPlayerOwner`) + their own assigned character (2026-08-14 fix — it used to return *only* the GM's assigned character, so `canSwitch` was false and the GM was **locked to one character** with a dead OPERATOR chip; the fix scopes the GM to the PCs — the meaningful set to operate/test as — not the whole world of NPCs; to operate as an NPC, assign it as `game.user.character`). `resolveOperator`'s default is unchanged (a GM with an assigned character still defaults to it; with none it stays null → force/adjudicate). The picker itself is **`OperatorSelect.runOperatorSelect`** (`scripts/ui/content/OperatorSelect.js` + `styles/components/operator-select.css`, `@import`ed after bank-heist.css) — a framework-free FUI promise modal in the SecurityConsole/BankHeist mould (appended into `host` so it inherits that viewer's `--ncm-*` preset): a **BIOMETRIC HANDOFF** panel (scanlines, corner brackets, per-card portrait scan-sweep, CPR role + live stat readout, ACTIVE/SELECT chips) whose pick runs a verify beat (progress fill → `IDENTITY CONFIRMED — NAME`, `login-success`) before resolving. Reduced-motion resolves instantly; ESC/backdrop/CANCEL abort.

**Player content writes need a GM online — guard BEFORE anything irreversible.** Entries live in GM-owned journals, so a player's `persistContentData` RELAYS; with no GM connected the write evaporates and the player still gets the payoff (a claimed-eddies flow credited wealth, failed to persist `claimed`, and could be re-claimed forever; ANALYZE announced the hidden payload that never got marked `sniffed`). **`canPersistContent()`** (SiphonFlow) returns false + warns when `!isGM() && !game.users.activeGM`; `runEddiesClaim` / `runAnalyze` / `runMalwareDeploy` / `runContentUnlock` all check it as their FIRST statement. `persistContentData` warns + returns false in the same case rather than silently dropping. (This is also the answer to "declassify/decrypt/deploy do nothing" — no GM was online.)

**ANALYZE — the player-facing file scan (`runAnalyze`).** A magnifier chip on **EVERY typed file** in the terminal detail + shard entry header (deliberately type-agnostic — a button that only appeared on trojans would BE the tell). Rolls vs the file's `sniffDV`: a disguised payload is unmasked (persists `sniffed: true`, same write as the GM REVEAL), a clean file reports "no anomalies" — so scanning is a real, sometimes-wasted choice. A failed probe on a booby-trapped disguise (`traceOnFail`) fires the trace. The GM's `payloadSniff` REVEAL survives as the force path. **List surfaces must label a disguised payload as its COVER type** — `displayContentType(msg, {isGM})` (ContentRenderers) does this; the terminal sidebar and shard index chips read type labels straight off `contentType` and announced "Payload" before the file was ever opened, blowing the disguise.

**Consumers:**
- **Shard viewer** — `ItemInboxApp._enrichEntry` → `enrichContent(entry, {isGM, interactive:true})`; `_buildIndexChips` → `buildIndexChip`; `item-inbox.hbs` wraps the partial with its corrupted/`isLocked` overlays (`{{else}}{{> content-body}}`).
- **Terminal viewer** — `TerminalViewerApp._detailContext` detects `msg.contentType` (or `msg.metadata.contentType`); when set & ≠ `message`, it runs `enrichContent(… , {interactive:false})` and exposes `isTypedContent` + `content`. `terminal-viewer.hbs` renders `{{#with content}}{{> content-body}}{{/with}}` for typed content, else `bodyHtml`.
- **Message viewer (Agent inbox)** — recovered/exfiltrated typed files render here too: `MessageViewerApp._getEnrichedMessage` mirrors the terminal's detection (`isTypedContent`/`content` → the shared partial in `message-detail.hbs`'s normal branch), the `--sp-*`→`--ncm-*` token block lives on `.ncm-app .ncm-viewer` (message-viewer.css §24, same as terminal.css), and the typed-content actions (`claimEddies`/`crackCred`/`credToggleSecret`/`syslogFilter`/`siphonFunds`) are wired in the delegated click router to the shared SiphonFlow/ContentRenderers helpers (persistence keys on `messageId`, so inbox pages resolve like shard/terminal pages). Note the name split: the flag-based eddies claim is `claim-eddies` (kebab), the typed-content claim is `claimEddies` (camel) — don't merge them. A typed message inside a *thread transcript* entry still shows "// No message body //" (known cosmetic gap).

**Storage:** typed content persists as top-level message flags `contentType`/`contentData` — added field-by-field to `MessageRepository.createMessage` + `_pageToMessage` **and** `TerminalService._buildMessageFlags` (the same build-field-by-field gotcha that drops `spam`/`recovered*`). Null = an ordinary message.

**CSS scope caveat (resolved):** the existing 6 types' CSS is `.ncm-app`-scoped in `item-inbox.css` (both viewers carry `ncm-app`, so it cross-applies) but reads bare `--sp-*` vars whose defaults live on the shard viewer root (`.ncm-app .ncm-item-inbox`). Fix: `.ncm-terminal-viewer` (terminal.css) now defines the full `--sp-*` token set mapped to `--ncm-*` with the same hex fallbacks — legacy typed content renders inside the terminal **and tints to the terminal preset** (presets override `--ncm-*` on the viewer root). Don't remove that block, and keep new types' shared CSS (`content-types.css`) on `--ncm-*` tokens only.

## Architecture: terminals (Terminal Expansion)

A **Terminal** is a converted Item you "log into" (an account-scoped mailbox), the **sibling of a Data Shard** (a file you crack open). Full design: `dev/design/TERMINAL-EXPANSION.md`. Phase 1 (MVP) is built; the content-type catalog, full method-card auth dialog, composer-grade compose, and tension/liveness features are Phase 2/3.

**Three-flag pattern, mirroring shards.** `flags.<MODULE>.{ isTerminal:true, terminalConfig, terminalState, journalId }` (`DEFAULTS.TERMINAL_CONFIG/STATE/ACCOUNT`). Messages live in **one linked journal** (`type:'terminal'`, `linkedItemId`, folder "Terminals") with each page tagged by `accountId` — `MessageRepository.identifyTerminal(journal)` finds it (flag-based; separate from `identifyInbox`, which returns null for terminals). `TerminalService` (constructed in `registerTerminalSystem.js`, ready/62) parallels `DataShardService` but **delegates** security/ICE/storage. Macro API: `game.nightcity.messenger.convertToTerminal/addTerminalAccount/addTerminalMessage/forceUnlockTerminal/relockTerminal`; launcher `game.nightcity.openTerminal(itemOrId)`.

**Accounts are the heart.** An account = login + mailbox + optional identity link: `{ id, username, displayName, email, masterContactId, actorId, portrait, isAdmin, hidden, security }`. `security` is the **byte-for-byte shared SECURITY_BLOCK** used by networks/shards (password/skill/keyitem/authLogic/lockout/ICE) — author it the same way, never fork the contract. Portrait resolves via `PortraitService.resolvePortrait` (linked master contact → actor → initials). `hidden` accounts aren't on the login card list — reachable only by typing the username via "Log in with another account" (secret login). **The GM always sees hidden accounts** (`getAccounts` includes them for `isGM()`), chipped **HIDDEN** (amber, `hiddenGm` in `_cardContext`) on cards/crt/roster/portraits — "hidden account still shows for me" as GM is by design, not a bug.

**Accounts as inboxes — the delivery mirror (2026-07-12).** A terminal account linked to an identity IS that identity's inbox on the machine: after normal delivery, `MessageService._deliverMessage` (always the GM client) calls `TerminalService.mirrorDeliveryToAccounts(recipientData)` — every account matching the recipient (`masterContactId === toContactId` · `actorId === toActorId` · `email === to` case/space-insensitive; hidden/net-gated accounts still match — those filters are read-time visibility, not ownership) gets a copy via `addAccountMessage` with the **original From + timestamp**, landing unread in the account's Inbox. So a PC replying to a terminal-linked contact shows up on that terminal. Three rules: (1) **the mirror gets its OWN messageId** (`<id>-t-<accountId>`, which doubles as the redelivery guard) — content mutations resolve pages by messageId across ALL journals (`applySiphonToJournal` takes the first hit), so a shared id would desync claim/siphon state between the inbox copy and the mirror; (2) **money never duplicates** (the exfil no-double-grant rule): mirrors carry `eddies: 0` + `_exfilContentData` neutralization — the recipient's real inbox copy is the canonical claim surface; (3) **spam-routed mail is not mirrored** (it would defeat the silent filter), and anonymous mail mirrors the recipient-facing masked clone. Open mailboxes refresh live: the viewer registers create/update/delete `JournalEntryPage` hooks (constructor, `Hooks.off` in `close()`) filtered to the linked journal **and mailbox state only** (a re-render during login/manual would wipe a half-typed password) — this also fixed the standing relayed-write lag. `_playExfilFx` now sets `_animationActive` for its duration so a wipe's own page deletions can't destroy the FX overlay mid-animation (`_debouncedRender` respects the flag). Logic sim: scratchpad `test-mirror.mjs` (18/18 through the real method).

**The TerminalViewerApp extends BaseApplication (NOT MessageViewerApp).** The bespoke `ncm-term__` template + state machine made forking the 5k-line viewer more coupling than reuse. It uses the AppV2 `actions` map (`action('_onX')` delegators). State machine: **boot → gate → login → manual → granted → mailbox** (`data-terminal-state` on the root; `_resolveEntryState` decides gate-vs-login post-boot). Source adapter: the mailbox feed is `TerminalService.getAccountMessages(item, accountId)` (standard message shape via `MessageRepository._pageToMessage`). The MessageViewer `_createMessageSource()` seam stays as the documented hook for a future shared base. **Layout gotcha:** the part root needs `.ncm-terminal-app .window-content { display:flex; flex-direction:column; align-items:stretch }` + `.ncm-terminal-viewer { width:100%; height:100% }` or it collapses to the far left — headless CSS verifies MUST nest as `.application > .window-content > viewer` (no fixed-size wrapper) to catch this. **Pane scroll survives re-renders** via a `_preRender` scroll memo (`_scrollMemo` — sidebar always restores; the detail pane only when the same message is still open): every data-action re-renders the whole part, so without it each click snapped both panes to the top. The scroll panes (`.ncm-term__detail/__login/__gate` and the sidebar's `.ncm-term__list`) pair `overflow-y:auto` with `overflow-x:hidden` (the phantom-horizontal-scrollbar gotcha). **The sidebar (`.ncm-term__side`) is NOT a scroller anymore** — it's a flex column (`overflow:hidden`): account switcher + folder strip (`.ncm-term__folds`, max-height 40% w/ internal scroll) + list label stay pinned, only `.ncm-term__list` scrolls (the `_scrollMemo` targets it), and the GM New-message button pins to the bottom. **Message rows are separated cards** (border + 6px gap, priority-colored left edge, `--urgent/--critical` modifiers): line 1 = pdot + From + short date (`formatCyberDate dateOnly`), line 2 = subject, line 3 = a one-line preview — a stripped-HTML body snippet, or the file-type tag (`CONTENT_TYPE_INFO` icon+label) for typed content. Headless-verified (`sidebar1.png`).

**GM bypass is EXPLICIT, not automatic.** `requiresAuth`/`checkNetworkAccess` bypass only when `terminalState.gmBypassed` is set (via force-unlock), mirroring shard `forceDecrypt` — so a GM sees locked accounts and can test the login (or force-unlock to skip). Don't re-add an `isGM()` auto-bypass; that made every account read "open" for the GM tester. Login cards show truthful lock status.

**Machine uplink (Layer 1) — the TERMINAL's own network, never the player's.** Three layers: Layer 0 = the existing reachability gate (`config.network` — "be on network X to reach this box", player's network, unchanged); **Layer 1** = the machine's own connection (`config.connection = { interfaces: [{networkId, preAuthorized, wired}], defaultNetworkId, allowPlayerSwitching, showSyncHint }` + `state.connection = { currentNetworkId, authenticated }`); **Layer 2** = content gating on that connection. Scene availability/dead zones deliberately don't apply to interfaces (a wired box works in a jam). Key mechanics:
- **Signal display is honest, wired is a flavor flag.** The mailbox-HUD bars light per the ACTIVE uplink's signal (`_uplinkContext` top-level `signal`/`sigLabel`; the network's configured `signalStrength`, app-wide thresholds 80/50/25) — they were four always-lit `<b>`s before ("CitiNet 75% but bars show 100"). Per-interface **`wired`** (config Network tab checkbox next to pre-authorized) = a physical LAN drop: signal reads 100, dropdown rows show `· wired`, the pill gets a small glyph, and **the HUD swaps the signal bars for a hardline icon entirely** (`.ncm-term__hud-wired`, `{{#if uplink.wired}}` branch). The wired glyph is **`fa-network-wired`, NOT `fa-ethernet`** ("looks like a comb" — dev). **Display/fiction only** — gating already ignores scene jamming for uplinks, so `wired` changes no mechanics.
- **`state.connection.currentNetworkId` uses the sentinel `'__DEFAULT__'`** (= follow `config.connection.defaultNetworkId`), NOT an absent key — whole-state rewrites (`_updateSession`, force-unlock) would otherwise convert "never set" into an explicit `null` and silently kill the boot default. `null` = explicitly disconnected (LOCAL ONLY). `TerminalService.getUplink` resolves sentinel → default → validates the network still exists AND is still an interface (else degrades to LOCAL ONLY). Logic sim: scratchpad `test-uplink.mjs` (12 cases).
- **Gating check is `networkVisibility`-shaped** (shard shape: `{restricted, allowedNetworks[], allowedTypes[]}`) via two service methods: `uplinkReaches(item, nv)` (raw, no GM bypass — used to badge **OFF-NET** chips for the GM) and `isContentVisible(item, nv)` (GM-bypassing enforcement). **Accounts** carry `account.network` — gated accounts **VANISH** for players (filtered in `getAccounts`, which also kills the secret-login path since `_resolveAuthAccount` goes through it); the GM keeps them with an OFF-NET chip. **Messages** carry per-message `networkVisibility` (persisted in `_buildMessageFlags` + read in `MessageRepository._pageToMessage` — the field-by-field gotcha, needed for composer edit-prefill); the mailbox filters them and shows a GM-toggleable **"N objects failed to sync"** hint (`showSyncHint`, counted within the current folder). **Exfil copies never carry `networkVisibility`** (a downloaded file is local — `createMessage` doesn't persist the key, `exfilToShard` doesn't pass it).
- **Network icons honor custom images**: the uplink dropdown rows and the network-gate cards render via `{{{ncm-net-icon theme}}}` (`_uplinkContext`/`_gateContext` expose the full `theme` block) — a network configured with `iconMode:'image'` + `customImage` in Network Manager shows its logo, like every other network list in the app. Wired interfaces keep the hardline-glyph override; `.ncm-net-icon-img` gets a fit rule (radius+padding) scoped to the terminal icon squares.
- **Switching**: the UPLINK pill (login hero — pre-login switching matters since gated accounts vanish — + mailbox HUD) opens a dropdown of interfaces (partial `templates/terminal/partials/uplink.hbs`, invoked `{{> … uplink align="right"}}` in the hero). GM writes via `TerminalService.setUplink`; **players relay** via `SOCKET_OPS.TERMINAL_NET_REQUEST` (no RESULT op — the flag write syncs and the viewer re-renders off an `updateItem` hook registered in the constructor, removed in `close()`, skipped during boot/granted animations). A non-`preAuthorized` secured interface opens a **join-challenge console** (`ncm-term__netauth` overlay; the NETWORK's own security block; lockout keyed `${itemId}::net::${networkId}`; skill bypass defaults Interface/DV15 so core nets always give players a roll path; GM gets a "join without credentials" row); success stamps `state.connection.authenticated[networkId]` — the machine remembers.
- **Mid-session uplink switch away from a gated account drops the session** — `_prepareContext` detects the now-unreachable account and bounces to login ("UPLINK LOST").
- The composer's Network-Visibility fieldset is **no longer hidden for terminal targets** (the `{{#unless isTerminalTarget}}` wrap is gone; terminal targets get uplink-specific hint copy).

**Auth runs through the shared method-card dialog.** Selecting a secured account (`TerminalViewerApp._onSelectAccount`, card/roster/portrait styles) opens `NetworkAuthDialog.showForAccount(item, accountId)` — the SAME dialog networks use, with password + skill (`SkillService.performCheck`) + key-item + **strict AND/OR** (`authLogic`) + lockout (`SecurityService`, keyed `terminalId::accountId`) + Black ICE (`failureMode==='damage'`). The dialog was **parameterized via a source-adapter seam** (`scripts/ui/NetworkManagement/SecurityAuthAdapters.js`): `NetworkAuthDialog._adapter` abstracts the four source-specific ops — `refresh()` (fresh source-shaped `{id,name,type,theme,description,security}`), `checkPassword()`, `checkSkill()`, `grant()` — so `_prepareContext`/handlers stay source-agnostic and `this.networkId` is just the SecurityService key (a network id, or `terminalId::accountId`). `NetworkAuthAdapter` preserves network behavior byte-identically (delegates to `networkService.authenticate*`/`grantAuth`, which own their sounds + `_authenticatedNetworks` grant + events); `TerminalAuthAdapter` reads the account's SECURITY_BLOCK directly (password/DV compare, plays the sounds itself, `grant()` is a no-op — the dialog's resolve drives `_loginSuccess`). On the terminal side GM bypass is per-login (the dialog's GM-bypass button resolves success; the account's `gmBypassed` force-unlock still short-circuits `requiresAuth` before the dialog opens). **The lockscreen/keypad inline-password paths and the secret-login ("another account") console stay as quick-entry shortcuts** — single-method, no strict AND/OR (typed-username secret login has no known account until submit). **Player session-state writes RELAY (2026-07-13):** login/account-switch/logout write `terminalState.sessions` on the world item, which players can't update ("User Player lacks permission to update Item" was the first real player-login bug) — `TerminalService._updateSession` emits `SOCKET_OPS.TERMINAL_SESSION_REQUEST` when `!isGM()` and the GM handler lands the write via `applySessionUpdate` (the uplink-relay pattern; no RESULT op — the flag write syncs back and the viewer re-renders off its updateItem hook; the viewer proceeds locally on its own `_accountId`, so the lag is invisible). The auth CHECK itself is client-side reads (the shard-relay trust model) — only the persistence relays.

**A login lasts as long as the WINDOW (2026-10-27, tester: "logged in once with the password, then got straight in forever — after closing, refreshing, even after the GM changed the password; only Force-Unlock + Relock reset it").** The viewer used to trust `terminalState.sessions[actor].loggedInAccounts`, a world flag nothing but Relock cleared. Now `TerminalViewerApp._unlocked` (Map accountId → the credentials it was unlocked against, `_credKey`) is the ONLY "already unlocked" answer: `_needsAuth(id)` = open account / GM force-unlock → no; else unlocked in this window against the CURRENT credentials. Closing the window, **Log out** (now ends the whole session), switching operator, a forced lockout and a refresh (the constructor clears a leftover record via `_clearStoredSession`) all end it; a GM change to the account's password or login methods kicks an open session back to login (`CREDENTIALS CHANGED`, via the updateItem hook). The stored session record still exists for footprints, residents and the admin view, but **never decides entry — don't call `terminalService.requiresAuth` from a login path.** Sim `dev/tools/sims/test-terminal-session.mjs` (18). Not live-tested.

**Login footprints + "N new since last login" (2026-08-14).** The machine records who logged into which account: `_updateSession`'s GM-side branch (the ONE chokepoint every session establishment lands in — same reason onLogin residents fire there) appends to **`terminalState.loginLog`** (`_appendLoginLog`: `{ts (game clock), accountId, username, operatorId, operatorName}`, capped `LOGIN_LOG_CAP` 50). Read via `getLoginLog(item)` / `getLastLogin(item, accountId)` (newest entry for one account). Consumers: (1) the ACCESS-GRANTED badge's **Last Login** field falls back authored `acct.lastLogin` → the previous footprint (`formatCyberDate`) → `FIRST LOGIN`; (2) an **"N new since last login"** line under the welcome (`_newSinceLastLogin` — counts non-sent/deleted/spam mail newer than the previous footprint; `.ncm-term__badge-newsince`); (3) Admin → Terminals detail shows **Recent Logins** (last 6, `recentLogins` in `TerminalsTab`, `.ncm-term-admin-footprint` rows). **Ordering matters:** `_loginSuccess` captures `this._prevLogin = getLastLogin(...)` **BEFORE** `recordLoginSuccess` writes — read-after-write would make the current login its own "previous" on the GM client while the relayed player client (whose write lags) saw the true previous; reading first makes both sides agree. **`relock` preserves `loginLog`** (it rewrites `terminalState` to defaults wholesale — the footprint log is the GM's audit trail and is explicitly carried over; note relock still wipes `residents`, pre-existing behavior). Sim: `dev/tools/sims/test-footprints.mjs` (13/13).

**Exfil animations + burn picker.** Both exfil actions play an immersive full-viewer overlay before the notification resolves (`TerminalViewerApp._playExfilFx(kind, label)` → `.ncm-term__fx`, ~2.2s, reduced-motion-aware, awaited so the toast lands after): **download** = a server→Agent packet stream (cyan, `send`→`receive` sounds, "SAVED TO RECOVERED"); **burn** = a data-chip with a sweeping laser + embers (amber/red, `shard-compile`→`shard-insert` sounds, "BURN COMPLETE"). The **Burn-to-Shard target picker** (`_pickBurnTarget`) is the shared name picker (Carried / World chips, shards tagged first, `admin:false` so it wears the player theme) — existing shards sort first with a SHARD badge (burning onto one appends an entry; onto anything else converts it), GM world items are tagged WORLD, and CPR sheet-abstraction item types (`NON_CARRIER_TYPES`: skill/role/criticalInjury/netarch) are filtered out so a character's skill items don't drown the gear.

**Boot icon has three sources** (`boot.iconMode`: `preset` | `fa` | `image`): the theme preset's icon, a custom Font Awesome class (`boot.faIcon`), or a custom logo image (`boot.imageUrl`). `_bootContext` resolves them; the TerminalConfigApp Boot tab has a source dropdown that shows only the relevant field with a live preview chip (manual listeners — `_onRender` `syncBootIcon`; the FA-vs-image row visibility + preview update). `terminal-boot.hbs` renders `<img>` for image mode else `<i>`. (The arasaka-workstation preset icon was `fas fa-building-shield`, which isn't a valid FA6 free glyph → empty square; corrected to `fas fa-building`.) Image mode also has **`boot.imageFrame`**: `framed` (default — the themed icon square, `object-fit:cover`) vs **`free`** ("No frame" checkbox in the image row) — the logo renders at natural aspect with no box/border (a `drop-shadow` glow instead), via `--free` modifiers on `.ncm-term__boot-icon` / `.ncm-term__hero-logo` / the config preview (`_resolveBootIcon` exposes `imageFree`; wide corp logos looked crammed in the 88px square). The `--free img` rules must stay AFTER the framed img rules (equal specificity — order wins).

**Modular mailbox folders (2026-07-11) — LOCATION semantics.** `TerminalService.getFolders(item)` resolves the sidebar's folder set, ordered **Inbox → customs → Saved → Trash**: system folder **names** stay in `config.folderNames` (the preset contract), while **`config.folders` = `{ icons: {inbox,saved,trash}, savedEnabled, trashEnabled, custom: [{id,name,icon}] }`** layers icons, Saved/Trash visibility, and GM custom folders on top (legacy configs resolve to exactly the old three). **A message lives in exactly ONE location — Trash (`status.deleted`) > Saved (`status.saved`) > its custom folder (`folder` flag) > the Inbox** (the mailbox filters, the composer prefill, and the Move-to selector all resolve in that order; sim asserts every message matches exactly one filter). The `folder` flag persists in `_buildMessageFlags` + reads in `MessageRepository._pageToMessage` (the field-by-field gotcha); **`_buildMessageFlags` translates the system ids** (`folder:'saved'|'trash'` → the status flag, folder cleared) and **honors caller `status.saved/deleted`** (they were hardcoded false — the same gotcha that ate spam). **Unknown/removed folder ids fall back to the Inbox** at read time, so deleting a folder never hides mail. Filing surfaces: the composer Entry Header's **Folder select** lists ALL folders (terminal targets only; `_collectEntryData` translates system locs into `entryData.folder`+`entryData.status` because the EDIT path writes entryData keys straight to page flags, bypassing `_buildMessageFlags`), and the viewer detail header's GM **"Move to…" `<select>`** (`.ncm-term__move-select`, `moveOptions` in `_detailContext` — hidden Saved/Trash still offered as targets; manual `change` listener in `_onRender`, the select-vs-data-action gotcha; replaced the old bookmark/archive toggle chips — the red confirmed permanent-delete stays). `updateAccountMessage` accepts `folder`. Editor = the config **Identity tab** "Mailbox Folders" section: per-row icon `<select>` (`TERMINAL_FOLDER_ICONS`, curated FA6-free; an off-list saved value is injected at the top so re-saving can't silently change it) + name + "show" toggle (saved/trash) or remove button (customs); **Add folder** builds rows DOM-only (`_onFolderAdd`, `data-folder-row`/`data-f-*` attrs are the save contract — `_onSaveConfig` walks the rows into `folderNames` + `folders`). Sim: scratchpad `test-folders.mjs` (16/16 — real `getFolders` + `_buildMessageFlags` via `prototype.call`, one-location invariant). The full-preset library snapshots `folders` too.

**Exfil + the Recovered folder.** `allowExfil` (master switch) gates "To my Agent" (copy to the player's inbox) and "Burn to Shard" (`DataShardService.addMessage` onto a target item). Both **carry the original `contentType`/`contentData`** so a typed file (spreadsheet/syslog/banking/…) renders identically at the destination — `exfilToShard` once hardcoded `contentType: MESSAGE` and dropped the data, which degraded burns to plain mail. Copies are built via `TerminalService._exfilContentData`, which **neutralizes money** (EDDIES copies get `claimed:true`, BANKING copies `allowSiphon:false` — the `eddies: 0` no-double-grant rule; without it, siphoning the original AND the copy duplicated eddies). The provenance body header is prepended to plain messages only (typed bodies never render). Both also **preserve the original From + timestamp**; the inbox copy carries the original recipient as `to` and a `metadata.recovered/recoveredFrom/recoveredTo/recoveredOrigTs` block so it lands in a dedicated **"Recovered" folder** in the MessageViewer (excluded from Inbox/Unread; rendered with the cyan `.ncm-viewer__recovered-chip`), never masquerading as the downloader's own mail. **`MessageRepository.createMessage` builds metadata field-by-field**, so the `recovered*` keys had to be added there explicitly (the same gotcha that drops `spam`/unknown keys). Service exfil is GM-only (GM-owned journals); **players relay** via `SOCKET_OPS.TERMINAL_EXFIL_REQUEST` → GM handler in `SocketHandlers.js` → `exfilTo*` → `TERMINAL_EXFIL_RESULT` notifies the requester (the shard-convert relay pattern).

**Theming** is per-terminal, scoped to `.ncm-terminal-viewer` via `data-terminal-preset` palette fallbacks in `terminal.css` (`TERMINAL_PRESETS`: arasaka-workstation/netwatch-unit/streetkid-bbs/retro-dos/ripperdoc — theme+security+boot+loginStyle+seed accounts). **`.ncm-terminal-viewer` carries a complete BASE `--ncm-*` palette** (brand defaults) so an unknown preset still renders self-contained — critical because `DEFAULTS.TERMINAL_CONFIG.theme.preset` is **`'classic'`, which has NO per-preset rule**; without the base palette every `--ncm-*` was undefined on a freshly-converted terminal, so the boot/login icon color resolved to inherited near-black on the dark bg = the "empty square" (an invisible glyph, not a missing one — headless can't reproduce it since FA doesn't load there). The per-preset rules are attribute selectors (higher specificity) so they still win for the five named presets. **Convert-to-Terminal re-renders the Items directory + open actor sheets immediately** (`ShardSheetOverride._refreshItemViews`, mirroring shard convert/remove) — previously the terminal badge/left-click-intercept only appeared after a manual refresh. The **boot-splash and login-hero icons share one resolver** (`_resolveBootIcon` → `{icon, imageUrl}`, honoring `boot.iconMode` preset|fa|image) so a configured custom icon/logo shows on both, never an empty box. **Custom (non-preset) theming is LIVE** — `ThemeService.applyTheme(prefs, targetEl)` is element-scoped (second param; document-global side effects like body classes/wallpaper only run when targeting `:root`, and `prefs.preset: null` skips the *player* THEME_PRESETS merge so a terminal can't inherit the Agent palette). The viewer applies `cfg.theme` on every render via `_applyCustomTheme` → the shared `buildTerminalThemePrefs(theme, presetKey)` (helpers.js): per key, saved override → `TERMINAL_PRESETS` palette → omitted (preset-attribute CSS / base palette shows through; a fresh terminal stores only `{preset:'classic'}` and renders unchanged). **All terminal.css `font-size`s go through `calc(Npx * var(--ncm-text-scale, 1))`** so the per-terminal Text Size slider works, isolated from the player's own scale. **The window-frame header follows the terminal theme too**: `_onRender` (and the config's `_applyThemeLive` preview loop) apply the same prefs to the **app root element** (`this.element`), because the `.window-header` (title spans `.ncm-term__title-*`, header controls) sits OUTSIDE `.ncm-terminal-viewer` and its `--ncm-*` lookups otherwise resolve from `:root` — the PLAYER's theme. `terminal.css` styles `.ncm-terminal-app .window-header` (bg-deep + primary-tinted border, control icons forced on both the button AND its inner `<i>` — the admin-theme window-chrome precedent) with `!important` against Foundry's frame styles. Don't move the viewer's inline vars UP to only the app root: the per-preset `[data-terminal-preset]` attribute rules on the viewer element would then out-specific the inherited customs. The config **Theme tab mirrors the player ThemeCustomizer** (it literally reuses the `.ncm-tc__` widget classes — theme-customizer.css's `.ncm-app`-scoped layout rules cross-apply; its `.ncm-tc`-root-scoped button/select resets are co-scoped to `.ncm-term-config`, keep the two selector lists in sync): a live-preview strip + font-preview box (class `.ncm-ttc-live` — `_applyThemeLive` applies the draft onto them AND any open viewer of this item), preset swatch-cards (incl. a "Classic" card = no-preset brand look; a card click stages palette+fonts after stripping prior overrides), a 9-key color-override grid with override dots + reset, font selects, and Text Size/Contrast sliders. **The theme save contract is `_themeDraft`, NOT form inputs** — theme controls are unnamed/data-keyed, mutate the draft via manual listeners (no re-render, so edits survive tab switches — the pre-rebuild bug was that any tab switch silently discarded them), `_onSave` persists the draft (`theme: deepClone(draft ?? cfg.theme)`) and clears it; closing unsaved reverts previewed viewers by re-rendering them. **Saved Themes** live in the world setting `terminalThemePresets` (`[{name, theme}]`, registered in SettingsManager) — save/apply/overwrite/delete rows + theme-only JSON import/export (`{ncmTerminalTheme:1, theme}` envelope, bare object accepted). The old "Apply preset" button is gone — a preset card click stages the full kit (see the login-styles section's `_presetKit` notes). **Boot** reuses the shard-style choreography; boot log lines auto-reveal via staggered CSS (`ncm-term-bootline`) so the splash is never empty. The **ACCESS GRANTED** state is the "credential keycard" design (`_grantedContext` → `ncm-term__badge*`), redesigned 2026-08-14 to a slicker **3-beat sequence** (~2.3s): (1) the keycard **materialises** from a top scanline sliver (`ncm-term-badgemat`, a `clip-path` reveal with **NO `forwards`** so it reverts to `none` at rest — otherwise a persisted `inset(0)` clips the card's box-shadow glow) while corner brackets draw in (`ncm-term__badge-brk`); (2) a single **reader line** (`ncm-term__badge-reader`) sweeps down the whole card once — each field's VALUE **types in** left-to-right (`clip-path`, staggered by inline `--r`, blinking cursor via `.ncm-term__badge-val::after`) and a **hairline** along the bottom edge (`ncm-term__badge-prog-fill`) fills primary→green in step; (3) the card settles to a **green validated edge-glow** (`ncm-term-badgevalid` pulses then holds) as a green **check badge pops onto the portrait's lower-right corner** (`ncm-term__badge-chk` — the photo is `overflow:visible` and the `<img>` self-clips via its own `border-radius`, so the badge lays ON TOP and isn't clipped), then **ACCESS GRANTED** rises (`login-success` sound; **2.7s** hold before mailbox — timings are hardcoded in the CSS delays, keep them in sync with the hold in `_onRender`). The reader + hairline + brackets all ride **`--ncm-primary`** (the machine's identity colour, never `--ncm-secondary`) so on a red-primary preset nothing sweeps foreign blue. Theme-aware (accents = `--ncm-primary`, verify = `--ncm-success`); mock `dev/mockups/terminal-login-animation-mock.html`, port preview `terminal-login-port-preview.html`. (This replaced a **busier** version — per-row checkmarks + an AUTHENTICATING→VERIFIED progress bar + a full-card scan light-bar + a VALIDATED chip all at once over ~3s — the dev found it too busy; don't reintroduce those, and don't bring back the earlier diagonal green rubber-stamp / RGB-glitch either.) The badge is driven by **per-account credential fields** (`TERMINAL_ACCOUNT`: `role`, `clearance`, `idNumber`, `classification`, `location`, `lastLogin`), all editable in the config's **Accounts → Credential Badge** section, each **falling back** to terminal metadata (classification/location) or a derived default (clearance ← `isAdmin`, idLabel ← email/username, lastLogin ← "FIRST LOGIN"). So classification (RESTRICTED/SECRET/TOP SECRET…) can differ per account. Wired through `_grantedContext` → `badge` + `_editorContext`/`_onSaveAccount` (`acct*` fields). `role` is now editable (it was previously only seedable via presets). **`_bootContext` must return the boot fields directly** (`{ icon, title, … }`), NOT wrapped in `{ boot: … }` — it's assigned `ctx.boot = this._bootContext(...)`, so a wrapper double-nests to `ctx.boot.boot` and the template's `{{boot.icon}}`/`{{boot.title}}` render undefined = **a blank glowing icon square + no title/subtitle on the boot screen** (the loading bar still shows since it has no data binding). This bug hid behind hand-written headless tests that hardcoded the icon class instead of going through the context — the lesson: verify data-bound values through the real `_prepareContext`, not just static markup. (The other state contexts use `Object.assign(ctx, this._xContext())` and so return `{ … }` maps; boot is the odd one assigned to a named key.)

**Login styles + machine personality (2026-07-10 batch).** `config.loginStyle` (one of `TERMINAL_LOGIN_STYLES`: `cards` default · `crt` · `roster` · `lockscreen` · `keypad` · `portraits`) drives the login screen's LOOK — same accounts/security/auth flow underneath, all styles read `--ncm-*` so the terminal theme applies. `terminal-login.hbs` branches on style flags from `_loginContext` (root carries `data-login-style`). **cards/roster/portraits** route secured picks to the SECURE SHELL console as before; **crt** is a full-screen phosphor tty whose account lines also route there (the console is already console-styled, so the fiction holds); **lockscreen/keypad** feature ONE account (`_focusAccountId`, default = `defaultAccountId`; mini-avatar row refocuses via `terminalFocusAccount`, open accounts log straight in) with **inline password entry** — `terminalInlineAuth` binds the account from `closest('[data-account-id]')` (the keypad wrapper carries it) and runs the normal `_onManualAuth` (same lockout/ICE; failures render `inlineError` on the login screen). Keypad digits are wired in `_onRender` (pad buttons write a visually-hidden `manualPassword` input + echo dots on the LCD; ⏎ submits; non-password accounts get an "other access methods" link to the console). More personality knobs: `config.login = { motd, welcomeText }` (MOTD banner under the hero / custom ACCESS GRANTED line via `_grantedContext.welcomeText`); `config.folderNames = { inbox, saved, trash }` (diegetic mailbox labels, blank = defaults — CASE FILES / EVIDENCE / PURGED; see the **modular folders** paragraph below — `config.folders` layers icons/visibility/customs on top, names deliberately stay here for preset compatibility); `config.screensaver = { enabled, idleSeconds, text }` (idle dim + drifting marquee — `_resetScreensaver` re-arms on every render and on any pointer/key inside the window via a constructor-registered document-capture listener removed in `close()`; real-time `setTimeout` is deliberate); `theme.wallpaper` + `theme.wallpaperStrength` (**two intensity tiers**, both scaled by the 5–100 Theme-tab slider writing `--ncm-wallpaper-strength` [stored 0–100, applied ÷100, default 25]: **full-screen states — login / secure-shell `.ncm-term__mwrap` / ACCESS-GRANTED `.ncm-term__granted` / network gate — render it as the base background** at `min(1, strength × 2.6)` ≈ 65% by default, per dev feedback "it should just BE the login background", while the **mailbox panes stay subtle** at strength × 0.8 ≈ 20%; the config Theme-tab preview strip `.ncm-tc__preview.ncm-ttc-live` gets a wallpaper `::before` too so the slider visibly works without an open viewer. **The ACTUAL "wallpaper doesn't work" root cause: a relative `url()` inside a CSS custom property resolves against the stylesheet that USES the var** — terminal.css → `/modules/…/styles/components/worlds/…` → silent 404 — NOT the document base; `applyTheme` now roots the path via `foundry.utils.getRoute` (typeof-guarded — the ImagePopout lexical-global lesson; honors a route prefix; bare `/` fallback outside Foundry). It always clears both vars when unset and escapes `'` in the url. **Layering: each wallpaper host gets `isolation: isolate` + a `z-index:-1` ::before** (paints above the host's own background, below ALL content — no `> *` position/z-index overrides), and host `position: relative` goes through **`:where()`** so it can never out-order an existing scheme — a flat `position:relative` list once overrode `.ncm-term__granted`'s `position:absolute; inset:0` and collapsed the ACCESS GRANTED card to the top of the flow. Verified end-to-end through the REAL ThemeService/buildTerminalThemePrefs in headless Chrome, 8/8 (`test-wallpaper2.html` / `wallpaper2.png` — URL resolution, tier opacities, centering intact)) and `theme.scanlines/flicker/glitch` (per-terminal effects — `data-term-scanlines/flicker` attrs set in `_onRender` + glitch riding `--ncm-glitch-intensity`, 0–100 in config ÷100 in `buildTerminalThemePrefs`). **Per-terminal sound sets are LIVE** — `config.soundSet` names a `TERMINAL_SOUND_SETS` entry (constants.js: `default` · `corp-chime` · `street-bbs` · `med-soft` · `mil-hardline` · `ghost-wall`); a set **remaps the terminal's sound ids onto other existing `SOUND_PATHS` assets with playback-rate + volume shaping** (no new audio files — the `playClick` Web Audio precedent, via the new public `SoundService.playStyled(id, {volume, rate})`, which short-circuits to `play()` at rate 1 and falls back un-pitched if Web Audio fails).
